# Serbian, C1

803 meanings: 602 ok, 164 collision, 33 quarantined, 4 duplicate synonyms in Serbian.
Translated 803 (v1 kept 0); A and B agreed on 424/803 (53%); accepted without adjudication 370/803 (46%); adjudicated 427 (a 225, b 87, other 111, unsure 4); level overrides 2.

Conventions: nouns in the nominative singular, adjectives masculine singular, Russian and Serbian verbs as the imperfective infinitive with the perfective partner in the note (`pf:`), Serbian Ekavian in Cyrillic, Russian with ё, English in British spelling. Notes: `g` gender (only where the ending doesn't show it; `pl` = plural-only), `pl` irregular plural, `pf` perfective partner, `past` English irregular forms. Hints are the sense notes shown with a translation where several meanings share a term. Statuses: `ok` (offered to learners of the language), `collision` (another meaning has the same word in this language: shown to its native speakers, not offered to its learners), `quarantined` (failed verification: never shown), counted apart when it is a `duplicate synonym` (another meaning has the same term in this language and no hint tells them apart: the other one keeps it).

| `premises_n` | premises (land and buildings owned by an organization) | простор | посебне просторије | other / other → — | quarantined | Простор is general space, and b is Ijekavian-flagged; the plural-only просторије is the established word for premises of an organization. / Простор means space; the plural-only просторије is the established word for an organization's buildings and grounds, in nominative plural. |
| `speculation_n` | speculation (guessing without enough information) | спекулација | нагађање | b / b → нагађање | ok | Guessing about the future without information is native нагађање; спекулација leans toward finance or conspiracy talk. / Plain guessing without enough information is нагађање; спекулација often means financial speculation, a different main sense. |
| `wicked_adj` | wicked (very bad or morally wrong) | зао | злочишки | a / a → зао | collision | Зао is the common adjective for morally bad deeds; злочишки is not an established word. / Зао is the plain common adjective for morally bad, fitting a wicked deed against a neighbor; злочишки is not a real word. |
| `inclusive_adj` | inclusive (including everything or everyone) | укључив | укључен | other / b → tie: first → укључен | collision | Укључив is barely attested and укључен means 'switched on/included'; инклузиван is the listed adjective for 'including everything or everyone'. / Price inclusive of tax is said with укључен porez; укључив is rare and not idiomatic in this sense. → tie: A price inclusive of tax is expressed with укључен; инклузиван is a social-policy term and укључив is rare. |
| `clan_n` | clan (large family or social group) | клан | породица | a / a → клан | ok | Клан is an established noun for a kin group, fitting the whole clan at dinner; породица is only family. / Клан is the established term for a large kin group; породица is too narrow for a whole clan at dinner. |
| `circulation_n` | circulation (movement of blood or air through a system) | циркулација | крвоток | a / a → циркулација | ok | Sense covers blood or air through a system; циркулација is general, whereas крвоток names only blood flow. / Циркулација covers both blood and air flowing through a system, as the sense states; крвоток is limited to blood. |
| `fraction_n` | fraction (part of a whole amount or number) | разломак | део | b / b → део | collision | The example means a small portion of salt, not a mathematical fraction; део is the common word, while разломак is arithmetic. / A small fraction of salt means a small part, so common део fits; разломак is the mathematical fraction only. |
| `integral_adj` | integral (completely necessary for completeness) | саставни | неопходан | b / a → tie: first → саставни | ok | Trust is integral to a relationship means indispensable; неопходан states that directly, whereas саставни only means component. / Саставни (as in саставни део) means an integral part of a whole; неопходан only says necessary, missing the part-of-whole idea. → tie: Саставни део expresses being an integral part of a whole, which неопходан alone, meaning merely necessary, misses. |
| `puzzle_v` | puzzle (to cause someone to feel confused) | збунити | збунити | other / other → збуњивати | ok | Both give perfective збунити; the imperfective infinitive for 'his silence puzzled everyone' is збуњивати. / Both answers repeat perfective збунити; the imperfective збуњивати fits silence that puzzled everyone and keeps the aspect rule. · judge: pass: збуњивати is the common imperfective verb for confusing someone, fitting his silence puzzling everyone. |
| `swap_v` | swap (exchange one thing for another) | заменити | разменити | other / other → мењати | collision | Both answers are perfective; imperfective мењати covers swapping seats, as in мењати места, and is the everyday verb. / Both answers are perfective; imperfective мењати covers swapping seats (мењати места) as the example uses it. · judge: pass: мењати is a common imperfective verb covering swapping seats or places, fits the example. |

| `robust_adj` | robust (strong, healthy, and unlikely to fail) | робустан | чврст | a / b → tie: second → робустан | ok | A resilient economy that resists crises is робустан; чврст means firm or solid and describes physical hardness. / Чврст is the common neutral word for a solid, resilient economy; робустан is a bookish loanword. → tie: Робустан is established in economic usage (робустан раст) for resilient strength; чврст suggests physical firmness. |

| `descent_n` | descent (movement downwards) | спуст | силазак | other / other → спуштање | ok | A plane's descent is a lowering in altitude; спуст suggests a ski run and силазак a climbing-down on foot, so спуштање fits. / Спуст suits ski runs and силазак people walking down; спуштање is the usual noun for a plane descending. · judge: pass: Спуштање is the common noun for downward movement, and спуштање авиона fits the aircraft example. |

| `exterior_n` | exterior (outer surface or side of a building) | спољашњост | екстеријер | a / a → спољашњост | ok | Спољашњост is the native noun for a building's outside; екстеријер is a technical loan from architecture and car design. / Plain common noun for the outside of a building; екстеријер is a technical loanword used mainly in design. |

| `divergent_adj` | divergent (moving or growing apart) | различит | разилазећи | unsure / a → tie: other → — | quarantined | Различит only means different and разилазећи is a participle, not a listed adjective; no established word clearly means moving apart. / Real adjective fitting friends' differing paths in life; b's разилазећи is a participle, not a dictionary adjective. → tie: Различит only means different; разилазећи is a participle; дивергентан is the established adjective for diverging. · judge: fail: дивергентан is a jargon calque of the English word; Serbian uses различит or који се разилази for diverging paths. |
| `antics_n` | antics (funny or silly behavior) | лудости | шале | other / other → лудорије | ok | Лудости is a rare plural of an abstract noun and шале means jokes; лудорије is the established plural for a cat's silly antics. / Лудорије is the established plural for playful silly behaviour like a cat's; лудости means follies and шале jokes. · judge: pass: лудорије is an established plural noun for silly, playful behavior, fitting the cat's antics. |
| `resent_v` | resent (feel angry about unfair treatment) | огорчавати | завадити се | other / other → tie: first → — | quarantined | огорчавати means to embitter someone and завадити се means quarrel; негодовати is the imperfective for feeling indignant at unfair treatment. / Огорчавати is transitive 'embitter' and завадити се is perfective 'quarrel'; замерати means to resent or hold something against someone. → tie: Imperfective замерати means resenting or holding something against someone; огорчавати is transitive embitter and завадити се perfective quarrel. · judge: fail: замерати means to hold something against someone or blame them, which differs from resenting unfair treatment; the gloss also contains a non-Serbian form осећать. |
| `synopsis_n` | synopsis (brief summary of a story or film) | синопсис | сажетак | a / a → синопсис | ok | Brief summary read before a film is a synopsis in Serbian too; сажетак is a general summary of any text. / Specific term for a short outline of a film or story plot; сажетак is a general summary of any text. |
| `conserve_v` | conserve (protect natural resources from waste) | чувати | чувати | other / other → штедети | collision | Чувати means guard or keep; штедети воду is the idiomatic imperfective for saving water during a drought. / Conserving water in a drought means saving it, so штедети; чувати means guard or keep and misses the economy sense. · judge: pass: штедети is the common imperfective verb for saving or conserving water and resources, fits the drought example. |
| `discard_v` | discard (throw away something unwanted) | одбацивати | бацити | a / a → одбацивати | ok | Imperfective infinitive with partner одбацити; b бацити is perfective and its gloss means reject. / B is perfective бацити; одбацивати is imperfective and means throwing away the unwanted. |
| `wrench_v` | wrench (twist or pull suddenly) | тргнути | окренути | other / other → — | quarantined | Both answers are perfective or off-sense; the imperfective трзати expresses a sudden jerking pull, as with a door. / Тргнути and окренути are perfective; imperfective трзати covers a sudden violent pull, as when wrenching a stuck door. · judge: fail: трзати means to jerk, twitch or yank repeatedly, not a single forceful wrench of a door open; the back-translation tug is weaker than the sense. |
| `gourmet_n` | gourmet (person who enjoys fine food) | гурман | густош | a / a → гурман | ok | Гурман is the established noun for a connoisseur of fine food; густош is not a Serbian word. / Гурман is the usual noun for a lover of fine food; густош is not established in Serbian in this sense. |
| `torment_n` | torment (severe mental or physical pain) | мука | мучење | a / a → мука | ok | Torment lasting for days is a state of suffering; мука names that pain, while мучење is the act of tormenting. / Everyday noun for prolonged severe pain, matching torment lasting days; мучење names the act of tormenting. |
| `torment_v` | torment (cause severe pain or distress) | мучити | мучити | a / a → мучити | collision | Noise tormenting someone all night is distress; мучити is imperfective, with измучити as the standard aspect partner for exhausting suffering. / Both give мучити; it is imperfective and covers noise tormenting someone, with измучити as the perfective partner. |
| `hostess_n` | hostess (woman receiving guests) | домаћица | домаћиница | b / a → tie: first → домаћица | ok | Домаћиница means a woman receiving guests; домаћица mainly means housewife, the wrong sense for welcoming at the door. / Домаћица is the Serbian word for a woman hosting guests; домаћиница is the Croatian-leaning form. → tie: Домаћица is the standard Serbian word for a woman hosting guests; домаћиница is the Croatian-leaning variant. |
| `impoverished_adj` | impoverished (very poor and lacking resources) | осиротели | сиромашан | other / other → осиромашен | ok | Impoverished region means made very poor, осиромашен; осиротели means orphaned, wrong sense, and сиромашан lacks the lost-resources meaning. / Осиротели means orphaned, a wrong sense; сиромашан lacks the idea of being reduced to poverty. Осиромашен is the standard word for an impoverished region. · judge: pass: осиромашен is an established adjective (from осиромашити) meaning impoverished, fits the region example. |
| `ingenious_adj` | ingenious (clever and original) | генијалан | изванредан | a / other → tie: first → домишљат | ok | Генијалан fits a clever, original solution; изванредан only means outstanding without the notion of inventive cleverness. / Генијалан means brilliant and изванредан outstanding; домишљат is the established adjective for clever, original problem solving. → tie: Домишљат is the established adjective for clever, resourceful problem solving; генијалан means brilliant and изванредан outstanding. · judge: pass: домишљат is the established adjective for resourceful, clever and original, fitting an ingenious solution. |
| `loom_v` | loom (appear as a large threatening shape) | назирати | приближавати се | other / other → tie: first → помаљати се | ok | приближавати се means approaching and назирати is transitive; reflexive назирати се describes a looming shape appearing in a doorway. / назирати means to observe or peek; приближавати се only means approach. Помаљати се is the established word for a shape emerging threateningly. → tie: Помаљати се describes a shape emerging in a doorway; приближавати се only means approach and назирати is about peering. · judge: pass: помаљати се is an established imperfective reflexive verb for emerging into view, fitting a looming figure. |
| `bearded_adj` | bearded (having facial hair on the chin) | брадат | бородат | a / a → брадат | ok | Брадат derives from брада and is the Serbian form; бородат is a Russianism not used in Serbia. / Брадат is the Serbian form from брада; бородат is the Russian-derived (борода) form and not used in Serbia. |
| `ornamental_adj` | ornamental (decorative rather than useful) | украсни | украсан | a / a → украсни | ok | Украсни is the dictionary adjective for purely ornamental things (украсна биљка, украсни предмет); украсан tends to mean attractive. / Украсни means serving as decoration, matching a purely decorative vase; украсан suggests richly adorned rather than non-functional. |
| `farce_n` | farce (absurd or ridiculous situation) | фарса | фарса | b / a → фарса | ok | Both give фарса; a trial being a farce is absurd rather than merely funny, which B's gloss captures. / Both identical; фарса is the established noun for a ridiculous situation like the trial, matching the figurative English use. |
| `negligent_adj` | negligent (failing to take proper care) | немарљив | непажљив | other / a → tie: second → немаран | ok | Failing to take proper care in duties is немаран; немарљив would read as not diligent and is not listed, непажљив as inattentive. / Немарљив means neglectful of duty, exactly the example; непажљив is only inattentive or careless in the moment. → tie: Немаран is the established adjective for neglecting duties; немарљив reads as not diligent and непажљив as merely inattentive. · judge: pass: немаран is the established adjective for careless and negligent, fitting failing in one's duties. |
| `forfeit_v` | forfeit (lose something as a penalty) | изгубити | изгубити | other / other → — | quarantined | Both answers are perfective изгубити; the imperfective губити is the established verb, and the penalty meaning comes from context like losing a match. / Изгубити is perfective and flagged; imperfective губити, as in losing a match or right by penalty, is allowed and fits. · judge: fail: губити only means to lose and carries no penalty meaning; forfeiting a match is predati or izgubiti bez borbe, so the sense is lost. |
| `relapse_n` | relapse (return to a bad habit or illness) | рецидив | повратак | a / a → рецидив | ok | Рецидив is the established noun for a returning illness or habit; повратак only means a return. / Рецидив is the established medical and addiction term for relapse; повратак alone means just a return, with no illness sense. |
| `explanatory_adj` | explanatory (intended to explain something) | објашњавајући | објашњавачки | a / a → објашњавајући | ok | Explanatory notes are notes that explain; the participial adjective is established, whereas објашњавачки is not a dictionary word. / Established participial adjective for notes that explain; објашњавачки is a rare, awkward formation. |
| `smug_adj` | smug (unpleasantly proud of oneself) | самозадовољан | задовољан | a / a → самозадовољан | ok | Smug carries unpleasant complacency; самозадовољан expresses that, while plain задовољан only means content. / Самозадовољан carries the unpleasant complacent pride of smug; plain задовољан only means content. |
| `bulky_adj` | bulky (large and difficult to move) | гломазан | збијен | a / a → гломазан | ok | Гломазан is the standard word for large awkward objects that are hard to carry; збијен means compact, nearly the opposite. / Гломазан means large and awkward to move, fitting a box too big for a car; збијен means compact, the opposite. |
| `conservatory_n` | conservatory (glass room attached to a house) | зимска башта | стаклена тераса | a / a → зимска башта | ok | A glazed room beside a house for lunch is called зимска башта; конзерваторијум is a music school. / Зимска башта is the established Serbian name for a glassed garden room attached to a house. |
| `crumble_v` | crumble (break into small fragments) | мрвити се | рушити се | other / other → ронити се | ok | For an old wall, ронити се means to crumble away in bits; мрвити се suits bread, and рушити се means to collapse. / A wall crumbling as it ages is ронити се; мрвити се suits bread-like things, and рушити се means collapse. · judge: pass: Ронити се is an imperfective reflexive verb, and зид се рони is a normal Serbian expression for a wall crumbling. |
| `malfunction_v` | malfunction (fail to work properly) | отказивати | неисправно радити | a / a → отказивати | collision | Отказивати is a single imperfective verb for a device starting to fail; неисправно радити is only a descriptive phrase. / Рачунар отказује is the idiomatic single imperfective verb for failing to work; the two-word phrase is clumsier. |
| `physique_n` | physique (form and strength of a person's body) | телесна грађа | градња | a / a → телесна грађа | ok | Athletic physique is body build; телесна грађа is the established phrase, while градња means construction of buildings. / Телесна грађа is the established phrase for a person's body build; градња alone means construction and is wrong. |
| `degrade_v` | degrade (to lower in quality or value) | деградирати | угрожавати | a / a → деградирати | ok | Pollution lowering quality of the environment is деградирати, usable imperfectively; угрожавати means threaten, a different sense. / Loan verb in -ирати is bi-aspectual and used for environmental degradation; b's угрожавати means threaten, a different meaning. |
| `harass_v` | harass (to bother or annoy persistently) | узнемиравати | задржавати | a / a → узнемиравати | collision | Узнемиравати is the established imperfective for persistently bothering classmates; задржавати means to detain or keep, a wrong sense. / Узнемиравати is the standard word for harassing people; b's задржавати means to detain or keep, wrong sense. |
| `unmanned_adj` | unmanned (without people on board) | беспилотни | непосећен | a / a → беспилотан | ok | Unmanned drone is беспилотни, the established fixed form; непосећен means unvisited, wrong sense for no people on board. / Беспилотни is the standard term for drones and vehicles without crew; непосећен means unvisited, a wrong sense. · judge: pass: Беспилотан is the standard Serbian adjective for unmanned aircraft such as a drone. |
| `contested_adj` | contested (disputed or challenged by others) | спорни | споран | other / other → оспораван | ok | Спорни is a definite form and споран means doubtful or controversial; the participial adjective оспораван means challenged by others, as for election results. / Both answers mean 'disputable/controversial'; the participial adjective оспораван matches 'challenged by others' as in contested election results. · judge: pass: оспораван is an established participial adjective meaning disputed or challenged, fits contested election results. |
| `gruesome_adj` | gruesome (very unpleasant and shocking) | грозан | грозан | other / b → tie: first → језив | collision | Језив is the common word for a shocking, gruesome scene; грозан mostly means awful or nasty and is weaker for a crime scene. / Both give грозан; b's gloss, very unpleasant and shocking, matches a gruesome crime scene better than a's dread-causing gloss. → tie: Језив is the common word for a shocking, gruesome scene such as a crime scene; грозан is weaker, meaning awful or nasty. · judge: pass: Језив is a common Serbian adjective for horrifying, fitting a gruesome crime scene. |
| `latent_adj` | latent (existing but not yet visible or active) | латантан | скривен | other / other → латентан | ok | Latent tension is латентна напетост; a misspells it as латантан, and скривен means hidden on purpose, not dormant. / A has the misspelling латантан; скривен means merely hidden. Латентан is the correct established word, as in латентна напетост. · judge: pass: латентан is a standard dictionary adjective meaning hidden or inactive, masculine nominative, fits latent tension. |
| `limitless_adj` | limitless (having no limits or boundaries) | безгранични | неограничен | other / other → безграничан | ok | Limitless growth potential needs the indefinite masculine form безграничан; A's безгранични is the definite form and неограничен means legally unrestricted. / Безгранични is the definite form, not the dictionary headword; неограничен means legally unlimited. Безграничан fits limitless potential. · judge: pass: Безграничан is a standard dictionary adjective for limitless, and безгранични потенцијал fits the growth example. |
| `reassurance_n` | reassurance (words or actions that remove doubt or fear) | уверавање | уверење | a / a → уверавање | ok | Reassurance is the act of removing doubt; уверавање names that action, while уверење means a belief or certificate. / Verbal noun for comforting someone and removing doubt; уверење means a conviction or an official certificate. |
| `dismal_adj` | dismal (very sad or depressing) | мрачан | тужан | a / a → мрачан | collision | Cold dismal weather is gloomy and bleak; мрачан covers that, while тужан means sad about a loss and suits people. / Cold dismal weather is gloomy; тужан means personally sad, not bleak conditions. |
| `unison_n` | unison (doing or saying something at the same time) | једногласје | унисон | b / b → унисон | ok | Singing in perfect unison uses the musical term унисон; једногласје means unanimity or single-voice agreement, a different nuance. / У унисон is the established idiom for singing together at once; једногласје means unanimity or single-voice music, a different sense. |
| `dismay_v` | dismay (to make someone feel shocked and sad) | запрепастити | депримирати | other / other → — | quarantined | А is perfective запрепастити; the imperfective запрепашћивати fits bad news shocking everyone, and депримирати means depress, not shock. / Both answers are perfective or wrong sense; the imperfective запрепашћивати is the proper form meaning to dismay someone. · judge: fail: запрепашћивати is a rare bookish imperfective meaning to stun; not a common word a learner should get for dismay. |
| `dismay_n` | dismay (shock and sadness at something bad) | запрепашћење | очај | a / a → запрепашћење | ok | Looking at news with dismay is shocked consternation; запрепашћење is the exact established noun, whereas очај means despair. / Exact established noun for shocked dismay at bad news; очај is despair, a stronger different feeling. |
| `paternal_adj` | paternal (relating to a father or his side of the family) | очев | очински | b / b → очински | ok | Очински covers the father's side and fatherly qualities; очев is only the possessive 'father's' and zipf 3.45 reflects the name use. / Очински is the relational adjective for paternal; очев is only the possessive father's, less fit for kinship wording. |
| `realist_n` | realist (person who accepts situations as they really are) | реалист | реалиста | b / b → реалиста | ok | Masculine nouns in -а like реалиста are the usual Serbian form in Serbia; реалист is the rarer variant. / Serbian takes the -иста ending for such persons, like оптимиста and песимиста; реалист is the Croatian-style form. |
| `commend_v` | commend (to officially praise someone for good work) | похвалити | похвалити | other / other → tie: second → похваљивати | ok | Both answers give perfective похвалити; the imperfective похваљивати keeps the formal praise sense of a teacher commending a student. / Both answers are perfective похвалити; the imperfective хвалити fits a teacher praising a student for good work. → tie: Imperfective of похвалити with the formal tone of a teacher commending a student; plain хвалити lacks that formality. · judge: pass: Похваљивати is the imperfective verb of похвалити, meaning to praise; fits the teacher example. |
| `timid_adj` | timid (shy and lacking confidence) | стидљив | побожан | a / a → стидљив | collision | Стидљив means shy and fits the child hiding behind his mother; побожан means pious and is plainly wrong. / Common adjective for a shy child hiding behind his mother; б's побожан means pious and is wrong. |
| `deceit_n` | deceit (action of deceiving someone) | превара | обмана | b / b → обмана | ok | Example 'his deceit was discovered' is the act of deceiving; обмана names the deception, while превара leans to fraud or scam. / Names the act of deceiving someone, as discovered in the example; превара leans toward fraud or a scam. |
| `formulate_v` | formulate (create or develop carefully) | формулисати | формулисати | a / a → формулисати | ok | Same word in both; it is used imperfectively in Serbian, so the perfective flag is not decisive, and A's gloss fits devising a plan. / Формулисати is biaspectual like other -исати verbs, so acceptable here, and standard for formulating a plan. |
| `ingenuity_n` | ingenuity (creativity and skill in solving problems) | домишљатост | изуметлост | a / a → домишљатост | ok | Домишљатост is the established noun for inventive problem-solving; изуметлост is not a recognized word. / Домишљатост is the established noun for inventive cleverness in solving problems; изуметлост is not a real word. |
| `itinerary_n` | itinerary (travel plan with details) | план путовања | рута | a / a → план путовања | ok | No single common word exists; the fixed expression names a detailed travel plan, while рута means only the route taken. / Detailed schedule of a trip as on a flight booking; рута is only the path or route taken. |
| `clutter_v` | clutter (make untidy with many things) | затрпавати | нагазити | a / a → затрпавати | ok | Затрпавати means to fill a space with piles of things, matching clutter the kitchen; нагазити is perfective and means to step on. / Imperfective with correct partner затрпати, meaning to fill a space with stuff; нагазити is perfective and means to step on. |
| `clutter_n` | clutter (untidy collection of things) | неред | нередак | a / a → неред | collision | Неред names an untidy state of things, as on a cluttered desk; нередак is not a noun in this sense. / Неред is a real noun for mess and disorder on a desk; нередак is not an established word. |
| `drawback_n` | drawback (disadvantage or negative point) | недостатак | мана | a / a → недостатак | collision | The main drawback being the cost is a disadvantage; недостатак fits, while мана means a flaw or defect in something. / Neutral word for a disadvantage like cost; мана means a flaw or defect inherent in something. |
| `ferocious_adj` | ferocious (very fierce or violent) | жесток | свиреп | b / b → свиреп | collision | Свиреп means fierce and savage, matching a ferocious bark; жестоко tends toward cruel or harsh, a different shade. / Свиреп describes fierce animals and attacks, matching the dog's bark; жесток leans toward cruel or harsh. |
| `bubbly_adj` | bubbly (cheerful and lively personality) | живахан | журљив | a / a → живахан | collision | Живахан means lively and cheerful, matching a bubbly personality; журљив means hasty, a different meaning. / Живахан describes a cheerful, lively personality; журљив means hasty or hurried, a wrong sense. |
| `conformity_n` | conformity (compliance to standards or norms) | конформизам | усклађеност | a / a → конформизам | ok | Social conformity is adapting to group norms, the established sociological term конформизам; усклађеност means technical compliance or matching. / Social conformity as a tendency to adapt to the group is конформизам; усклађеност means technical compliance with regulations. |
| `frosty_adj` | frosty (very cold with frost) | мразовит | мразан | b / a → tie: second → мразовит | ok | Мразан is the short common adjective in мразно јутро for a frosty morning; мразовит is rarer and more literary. / Мразовит is the usual adjective for a frosty morning; мразан is rarer and tends toward icy or chilling. → tie: Мразовит дан or јутро is the usual adjective for a frosty morning; мразан is rarer and tends to icy or chilling. |
| `perpetuate_v` | perpetuate (make something continue indefinitely) | одужавати | одржавати | b / b → одржавати | collision | Одржавати keeps stereotypes alive in this sense; a's одужавати means to recover from illness. / Одржавати means to keep something going, as media keep stereotypes alive; одужавати means to recover from illness. |
| `resonate_v` | resonate (evoke a strong emotional response) | одјекивати | резонирати | unsure / b → tie: second → резонирати | ok | Одјекивати means to echo physically and резонирати is an uncertain loanword; no established single verb clearly means evoking an emotional response. / Резонирати carries the figurative sense of a story echoing in readers' feelings; одјекивати mainly means a sound echoing. → tie: Резонирати carries the figurative sense of a story echoing in readers' feelings; одјекивати mainly describes sound echoing. |
| `concur_v` | concur (to agree with an opinion or decision) | слагати се | слагти се | a / a → слагати се | collision | A is spelled correctly and imperfective; B is a misspelled perfective form of the same verb. / Imperfective reflexive verb for agreeing with an opinion; b's слагти се is a misspelling and means to defer. |
| `wares_n` | wares (goods offered for sale) | робе | роба | b / b → роба | collision | Goods for sale is the singular collective роба; A's plural робе is not the standard form for market goods. / Роба is the standard collective noun for goods on sale; a's плурал робе is rare and means different kinds of goods. |
| `divergence_n` | divergence (difference between two things) | разлика | разилажење | b / b → разилажење | ok | Divergence of opinion is a parting of views, which разилажење expresses; разлика is just a difference without the moving-apart idea. / Matches divergence of opinion as growing apart; a's разлика is a generic difference without the splitting-apart sense. |
| `rapport_n` | rapport (close and harmonious relationship) | склад | добар однос | b / b → добар однос | ok | They built a strong rapport maps to добар однос; склад is harmony in music or ideas, not a person-to-person bond. / Serbian lacks a single word for rapport, so the short phrase fits; склад means general harmony, not a relationship between people. |
| `rudimentary_adj` | rudimentary (basic or elementary level) | основно | основни | b / b → основни | collision | Rudimentary skills are основне вештине; A gives the adverb form основно, but a modifying adjective is needed. / Adjective required in masculine nominative; a's основно is the neuter or adverbial form, not the dictionary form. |
| `fatality_n` | fatality (death caused by an accident or disaster) | смртни случај | жртва | a / a → смртни случај | ok | Смртни случај names a death in an accident, as in no fatalities in the crash; жртва also covers the injured, so it is broader. / Нема смртних случајева is the standard news wording for fatalities in a crash; жртва is broader and includes the injured. |
| `unsustainable_adj` | unsustainable (not able to be maintained long term) | неодржив | неодржив | a / a → неодржив | ok | Both give same term; неодржив is the established word for unsustainable growth, and a's gloss adds the long-term idea. / Both give неодржив, the standard word for unsustainable growth; a's gloss explicitly includes the long-term nuance. |
| `closeness_n` | closeness (strong emotional connection) | блискост | близина | a / a → блискост | ok | Блискост means emotional intimacy, as in the example; близина mostly means physical proximity. / Блискост is the abstract emotional nearness of people; близина mostly means physical proximity. |
| `effortless_adj` | effortless (done without difficulty or effort) | беспрекорно | лаган | other / other → лак | collision | Беспрекорно means flawless and is an adverb; лаган means gentle or light, while лак is the adjective for something done easily. / A's беспрекорно means flawless and is an adverb; лаган means light or slow; лак is the common adjective for easy, effortless. · judge: pass: Лак is the common adjective for easy and light, and изгледати лако fits the dance example, though without nuance. |
| `ghostly_adj` | ghostly (like a ghost or pale and eerie) | духован | привидан | other / other → сабласан | ok | Ghostly light is eerie and pale; сабласан means ghostlike, while привидан means merely apparent and духован means spiritual. / Духован is not an established word and привидан is rare; сабласан is the dictionary adjective for eerie, ghostly light. · judge: pass: сабласан is an established adjective meaning ghostly or eerie, masculine nominative, fits the ghostly light. |
| `sedentary_adj` | sedentary (involving little physical activity) | седећи | седентаран | b / other → tie: second → седентаран | ok | Седентаран (седентарни начин живота) is the established term for little physical activity; седећи only means sitting. / Standard collocation is седелачки начин живота; седећи means sitting and седентаран is a jargon borrowing. → tie: Седентарни начин живота is the standard health term for little physical activity; седећи only means sitting. |
| `tranquil_adj` | tranquil (calm and peaceful) | спокојан | миран | b / b → миран | collision | Миран is the common neutral adjective for a calm, peaceful lake; спокојан is more often said of people or feelings. / Миран is the common word for a calm lake at dawn; спокојан usually describes a person's state of mind. |
| `astray_adv` | astray (away from the correct path or direction) | залутало | скретајући | unsure / unsure → — | quarantined | Залутало is a participle form and скретајући a gerund; neither is an established adverb, and no clear single Serbian adverb exists. / Залутало is a neuter participle and скретајући a present participle, neither an adverb; Serbian has no established single adverb for astray, only phrases like на странпутици. |
| `innovate_v` | innovate (to introduce new methods or ideas) | иновирати | инновирати | a / a → иновирати | ok | Иновирати is imperfective and correctly spelled; b has a Russian-style double н and is flagged perfective. / Иновирати is the correctly spelled established verb, usable imperfectively; инновирати has a doubled n and is misspelled. |
| `quirk_n` | quirk (unusual or unexpected habit or feature) | чудност | карактеристика | other / other → особеност | ok | A funny habit of speaking is a peculiarity, особеност; чудност implies mere strangeness and карактеристика lacks the oddity. / Чудност is rare and карактеристика is too neutral; особеност is the established noun for a peculiar trait such as a speech habit. · judge: pass: особеност is an established noun for a peculiar trait or habit, fitting a quirk of speaking. |
| `scorn_n` | scorn (strong dislike or disrespect) | презир | подругивање | a / a → презир | collision | Презир is the established noun for a contemptuous look; подругивање means mockery, a different sense. / Презир names contemptuous disdain, matching the look she gave him; подругивање is mocking, a different act. |
| `tantrum_n` | tantrum (sudden outburst of anger or bad temper) | бес | истапа | other / other → напад беса | ok | Бес alone is general rage, not a sudden outburst; истапа is not a real word. Напад беса is the fixed expression. / A child's tantrum: бес alone is just rage and истапа is not a real word; напад беса is the set expression. · judge: pass: напад беса is a standard fixed expression for a sudden outburst of anger, with no single common word available. |
| `upheaval_n` | upheaval (sudden and dramatic change or disturbance) | превирање | преокрет | a / a → превирање | collision | Политичка превирања is the standard phrase for political upheaval and confusion; преокрет means a reversal or turnaround. / Превирање is the established noun for social or political ferment and upheaval; преокрет means a turnaround or reversal. |
| `neutralise_v` | neutralise (to make something ineffective or harmless) | неутралисати | неутралисати | a / a → неутралисати | ok | Established biaspectual verb meaning to render harmless, fitting poison; b's gloss 'make neutral' misses the sense. / Established verb, treated as biaspectual in Serbian, so the flag is moot; a's gloss учинити безопасним fits poison, b's 'make neutral' misses it. |
| `relinquish_v` | relinquish (to voluntarily cease to keep or claim; give up) | одрицати се | одустати | a / a → одрицати се | ok | Imperfective одрицати се fits giving up control voluntarily; b's одустати is perfective and means give up an attempt. / Одрицати се is imperfective and means voluntarily giving up control or a claim; b's одустати is perfective and means quitting. |
| `dispense_v` | dispense (to distribute or provide something to people) | издавати | распоредити | other / a → tie: second → издавати | ok | Издавати suggests issuing documents and распоредити is perfective and means arrange; делити is the common imperfective for a machine handing out samples. / Imperfective with partner; machine giving out samples fits издавати, while B is perfective распоредити. → tie: Imperfective, and machines giving out items (банкомат издаје) fits; распоредити is perfective and means arrange. |
| `occupant_n` | occupant (a person who lives or works in a place) | становник | станар | b / b → станар | ok | Occupants of an evacuated building are its residents, which станар names; становник means inhabitant of a town or country. / Станар is the everyday word for a person living in a building, as in evacuated building occupants; становник means inhabitant of a town. |
| `refurbishment_n` | refurbishment (the process of renovating or redecorating a building) | реновирање | обнова | a / a → реновирање | ok | Closing a hotel for refurbishment is 'због реновирања'; обнова is broader and also means revival or new clothes. / Затворен због реновирања is the natural phrase for a closed hotel; обнова suggests broader rebuilding or revival. |
| `sprout_v` | sprout (grow or develop quickly) | ницати | клицати | a / a → ницати | ok | Ницати is the imperfective verb (partner никнути), so the flag is mistaken; fits hair appearing on a chin, unlike клицати. / Ницати is imperfective (partner никнути, so the flag is wrong) and fits hair appearing and growing; клицати is not a word for this. |
| `sprout_n` | sprout (small new growth on a plant) | изданак | клијак | a / a → изданак | ok | A sprout emerging from soil is a young shoot, изданак; клијак is a rare form for germinating seed. / Изданак is the common word for a young shoot emerging from soil; клијак is rare and means germ-like sprout. |
| `compulsion_n` | compulsion (strong urge to do something) | принуда | присила | other / other → порив | collision | Checking a lock is an inner urge; принуда and присила both mean external coercion, while порив is the established word for urge. / Принуда and присила both mean external coercion; порив is the inner urge, fitting a compulsion to check the lock. · judge: pass: порив is an established noun for a strong inner urge, acceptable for feeling a compulsion to check the lock. |
| `flicker_n` | flicker (brief unsteady light or movement) | трептај | треперење | b / b → треперење | ok | Треперење names an unsteady wavering light, matching flicker; a's трептај is more a blink or tremor. / Треперење names an unsteady, wavering light, matching 'brief unsteady light'; трептај is closer to a blink or tremor. |
| `oblige_v` | oblige (force or require someone to act) | обавезивати | обавезати | a / a → обавезивати | ok | Imperfective обавезивати with partner обавезати suits 'the law obliges us'; b gives the perfective обавезати, which is disallowed. / Imperfective with обавезати as partner; b is perfective, which breaks the aspect rule. |
| `tangle_n` | tangle (messy knot of strings or wires) | запетљај | запутаница | unsure / unsure → — | quarantined | Запетљај and запутаница are not clearly established nouns for a tangle of wires; Serbian has no secure single standard word here. / запетљај and запутаница are not clearly listed for a tangle of wires; no confident established single noun, so unsure. |
| `tangle_v` | tangle (to become twisted together in a mess) | запетљавати | запутати се | other / other → tie: second → заплитати се | ok | The sense is intransitive (headphones tangled), so reflexive imperfective запетљавати се is needed; a lacks се, b is perfective. / Headphones getting tangled needs a reflexive imperfective; запутати се is perfective and запетљавати lacks се; заплитати се is standard. → tie: Headphones getting tangled is intransitive; reflexive imperfective заплитати се is the standard verb, while запутати се is perfective. · judge: pass: Заплитати се is the imperfective reflexive verb for becoming tangled, fits headphones in a bag. |
| `motley_adj` | motley (made of different types or people) | шаран | разнородан | other / b → tie: second → шаролик | ok | A motley crowd means a mixed, varied crowd; шаролик is the established word for diverse, while шаран is a fish or a pied coat and разнородан is dry. / A crowd of mixed types is разнородна; шаран is archaic colorful-patterned and clashes with the fish шаран, carp. → tie: Шаролика гомила is the natural phrase for a mixed, varied crowd; шаран is a carp and разнородан is dry and abstract. · judge: pass: шаролик is a common adjective for varied, mixed, colorful, and applies to a motley crowd. |
| `symptomatic_adj` | symptomatic (showing signs of a problem or condition) | симптоматичан | симптоматски | a / a → симптоматичан | ok | Anger symptomatic of stress means indicative of it; симптоматски means relating to symptoms, as in symptomatic treatment. / Means indicative of an underlying condition, matching anger symptomatic of stress; симптоматски means relating to symptom treatment. |
| `tact_n` | tact (skill in handling difficult situations politely) | такт | дипломатија | a / a → такт | ok | Handling a matter with tact is exactly такт; дипломатија means the field of diplomacy and is a different sense. / Established noun for delicate handling of people; дипломатија means diplomacy as a field or policy, not personal tact. |
| `elitist_n` | elitist (a person who believes their group is superior) | елитист | елитиста | b / b → елитиста | ok | Serbian forms agent nouns in -иста (терориста, активиста); елитист follows the Croatian pattern. / Serbian person nouns in -ista (комуниста, тероријста) are masculine ending in -а; елитист is the Croatian-style form. |
| `shortlist_v` | shortlist (choose a small group as best candidates) | уврстити на ужи списак | укуцати у ужи избор | other / other → tie: first → уврштавати у ужи избор | ok | Both answers are perfective phrases; ужи избор is the established collocation and увршћивати is the imperfective, giving the required aspect. / Both answers are perfective phrases; Serbian has no single verb, and ужи избор is the established term, so the imperfective phrase уврштавати у ужи избор is used. → tie: Both candidates perfective; уврштавати is the standard Serbian imperfective of уврстити, and ужи избор is the set collocation. · judge: pass: уврштавати у ужи избор is the established fixed phrase with imperfective verb, as Serbian has no single word. |
| `antiquated_adj` | antiquated (very old and no longer useful) | антикни | застарео | b / b → застарео | collision | Застарео means outdated and no longer useful, fitting the slow system; антикни means ancient or antique, a different sense. / Застарео means outdated and no longer useful, as for a slow system; антикни means antique or ancient and has a positive tone. |
| `craving_n` | craving (strong desire for food or thing) | жудња | жеља | a / a → жудња | ok | Жудња is a strong urge for something, fitting the chocolate craving; жеља is only a general wish. / Жудња is a strong urge for something, as in a chocolate craving; жеља is only an ordinary wish. |

| `sombre_adj` | sombre (dark, dull, or gloomy in appearance) | мрачан | тамно | a / other → tie: first → суморан | ok | Мрачан means dark and gloomy, fitting a sombre grey sky; B is an adverb or neuter form meaning dark-coloured only. / A sky that is sombre and grey is суморан; мрачан means dark or sinister, and b's тамно is an adverb of colour. → tie: Суморан describes a dull grey gloomy sky exactly; мрачан leans to dark or sinister, тамно is only colour. · judge: pass: суморан is the common adjective for gloomy, dull, grey weather, matching the sombre sky. |
| `poise_n` | poise (calm and controlled manner) | присебност | самопоуздање | a / a → присебност | ok | Присебност means composure and presence of mind in a crisis, matching handling it with poise; самопоуздање is self-confidence. / Присебност means composure and presence of mind under pressure, fitting the crisis example; самопоуздање is self-confidence, a different sense. |
| `sanctity_n` | sanctity (sacredness or holiness) | светиња | светост | b / b → светост | ok | Sanctity of life is the abstract quality of being holy; светиња is a concrete sacred object or shrine. / Светост живота is the fixed phrase for the sanctity of life; светиња means a holy object or place, not the quality. |
| `alienate_v` | alienate (make someone feel isolated or estranged) | отуђивати | удалити | a / a → отуђивати | ok | Imperfective with partner отуђити; established verb for estranging people, whereas удалити is perfective and means removing or distancing. / Imperfective with partner отуђити, which fits turning friends away; b is perfective and удалити means remove. |
| `magnetism_n` | magnetism (personal charm or appeal) | магнетизам | шарм | a / a → магнетизам | ok | Лични магнетизам is an established figurative use matching the example; шарм means charm and lacks the drawing-in force. / Магнетизам is listed for personal appeal, mirroring the English figurative use; шарм means charm, a different word. |
| `stuffy_adj` | stuffy (unventilated and warm) | затворен | дуговит | other / other → загушљив | ok | A stuffy room after lunch is загушљив; затворен only means closed and дуговит is not an established adjective. / Загушљив is the established adjective for a room with stale warm air; затворен only means closed and дуговит is not a word. · judge: pass: загушљив is a standard adjective for airless, oppressive rooms, masculine nominative, fits the example. |
| `uniformity_n` | uniformity (state of being identical or consistent) | једноличност | једноликост | other / b → tie: first → уједначеност | ok | Impressive design uniformity is positive consistency; једноличност and једноликост both suggest dull monotony, while уједначеност means evenness and consistency. / Једноликост means sameness of form as in the design example; једноличност leans toward monotony, which would mislead the learner. → tie: An impressive design uniformity is positive consistency, which уједначеност names; једноличност and једноликост suggest dull monotony. · judge: pass: Уједначеност is an established noun for evenness and consistency, matching the uniformity of a design. |
| `methodical_adj` | methodical (done in a careful and organized way) | методичан | педантан | a / a → методичан | ok | Methodical approach to work means systematic and organized; педантан adds a fussy, negative nuance that the sense lacks. / Методичан means systematic and careful, matching her approach to work; педантан adds a fussy, negative nuance. |
| `tranquility_n` | tranquillity (state of calm and peace) | спокојство | мирноћа | a / a → спокојство | ok | Спокојство is the established noun for calm peace, as in a garden's atmosphere; мирноћа is rare and sounds odd. / Established noun for peaceful calm in a garden; мирноћа is rare and sounds unnatural. |
| `adjoining_adj` | adjoining (next to and connected to) | суседни | суседан | a / a → суседни | collision | Суседни is the dictionary adjective for neighbouring or adjacent rooms; суседан is not the normal form. / Суседни is the usual form in phrases like adjoining room (суседна соба); b's суседан is rare in this attributive use. |
| `gusto_n` | gusto (enthusiasm and energy) | жудност | ентузијазам | b / b → ентузијазам | ok | Eating with gusto expresses enthusiasm; жудност is not an established word, so the established noun ентузијазам is safer. / Жудност means craving or greed, wrong for eating with gusto; ентузијазам is an established noun covering enthusiasm and energy. |
| `overalls_n` | overalls (protective work clothing) | комбинезон | радни костим | a / a → комбинезон | ok | Комбинезон is the established single word for a painter's overalls; радни костим is a descriptive phrase for work suit. / Комбинезон is the common one-word term for work overalls worn while painting; радни костим sounds like a uniform suit. |
| `staggering_adj` | staggering (extremely shocking or surprising) | запањујући | шокирајући | a / a → запањујући | ok | A staggering cost is astonishingly large; запањујући conveys amazement, while шокирајући stresses offence or trauma. / Запањујући suits a shockingly large cost; шокирајући has a more emotional, scandalous tone and is a calque-like borrowing. |
| `succulent_adj` | succulent (juicy and tender to eat) | сочни | сочан | b / b → сочан | ok | Dictionary masculine nominative form; a lists the definite form сочни, which is not the lemma. / Indefinite masculine dictionary form fits a juicy steak; сочни is the definite form, not the lemma. |
| `sprawl_n` | sprawl (spread out over a large area) | ширење | расуљеност | a / a → ширење | ok | Urban sprawl is spreading over a large area, ширење; b's расуљеност is not an established word. / Ширење names spread over an area (ширење града); расуљеност is not a dictionary word with this meaning. |
| `sprawl_v` | sprawl (sit or lie with limbs spread out) | завалити се | развући се | other / other → tie: second → излегати се | ok | Both answers are perfective (завалити се, развући се); imperfective пружати се means stretching out limbs on a sofa. / Both answers are perfective; излегати се is the established imperfective for lounging sprawled out on a sofa, partner излегнути се. → tie: Imperfective излегати се is the established verb for lounging stretched out on a sofa, unlike the perfective candidates. · judge: pass: излегати се is the standard reflexive imperfective verb for lounging with limbs spread out, as on a sofa. |
| `wayside_n` | wayside (side of a road or path) | крај пута | ивичњак | a / a → крај пута | ok | Wildflowers by the wayside is крај пута; ивичњак means a curb, a concrete edge, which is wrong. / Крај пута is the natural phrase for the edge of a road where wildflowers grow; ивичњак means a curb stone. |
| `daydream_n` | daydream (pleasant thoughts while awake) | сањарење | сањарија | b / b → сањарија | ok | Example uses a countable daydream; сањарија is the concrete noun, while сањарење names the activity. / Countable noun for one daydream, as in lost in a daydream; сањарење names the activity of daydreaming. |

| `exuberant_adj` | exuberant (full of energetic excitement) | разигран | експресиван | other / a → tie: second → раздраган | ok | Разигран means playful and експресиван means expressive; раздраган is cheerfully excited, fitting a loudly cheering crowd. / Разигран describes a high-spirited, lively crowd; експресиван means expressive, a different sense. → tie: Раздраган means cheerfully, energetically excited, fitting a loudly cheering crowd; разигран suggests playful, and експресиван means expressive. · judge: pass: Раздраган is a real, common adjective for high-spirited excitement, fits the cheering crowd. |
| `fanciful_adj` | fanciful (imaginative and unrealistic) | маштовит | фантазирани | a / a → маштовит | ok | A fanciful story idea is маштовита idea; B фантазирани is a participle not used as an established adjective. / Маштовит is a real adjective for imaginative ideas; фантазирани is not an established Serbian adjective. |
| `pounce_v` | pounce (jump or move suddenly and quickly) | скочити | скочити | other / other → tie: second → — | quarantined | Both answers give perfective скочити; the imperfective скакати is the required form, and it covers a cat leaping on a mouse. / Скочити is perfective and plain jumping; насртати is imperfective and means to lunge at suddenly, as a cat on a mouse. → tie: Imperfective насртати means lunging suddenly at prey, as a cat on a mouse; скочити is perfective plain jumping. · judge: fail: насртати means to attack or assail someone, not to jump suddenly; the gloss contradicts the term, and the sense is the leap itself. |

| `restorative_adj` | restorative (helping you recover health or strength) | повратни | лековит | other / other → окрепљујући | ok | Повратни means returning or reflexive, and лековит means medicinal; окрепљујући (sleep) fits restoring strength in the example. / Повратни means return or reflexive, and лековит means medicinal; окрепљујући is the usual adjective for restorative sleep. · judge: pass: Окрепљујући is an established adjective for restoring strength, and окрепљујући сан fits restorative sleep. |
| `honk_v` | honk (make a loud horn sound) | трубити | сигналисти | a / a → трубити | ok | Трубити is the common imperfective for sounding a car horn; сигналисти is not an established verb. / Трубити is the imperfective everyday verb for sounding a car horn; сигналисти is a malformed, non-existent infinitive. |
| `enterprising_adj` | enterprising (showing initiative and resourcefulness) | предузимљив | предузетан | a / a → предузимљив | ok | Young man showing initiative and resourcefulness is предузимљив, the more common adjective; предузетан is rarer. / Предузимљив is the usual word for a person showing initiative and resourcefulness; предузетан is rarer and more bookish. |
| `inexcusable_adj` | inexcusable (impossible to justify or forgive) | неопростив | неоправдан | other / a → tie: first → неопростив | ok | Behavior that cannot be forgiven is непростив, the Serbian standard form; неопростив is Croatian-leaning and неоправдан only means unjustified. / Неопростив means cannot be forgiven or excused, matching inexcusable behavior; неоправдан only says unjustified, without the moral weight. → tie: Неопростива грешка is common in Serbian; the word means cannot be forgiven or excused, fitting inexcusable behavior. |
| `unplug_v` | unplug (disconnect from an electrical socket) | искључити | искључити | other / other → — | quarantined | Both answers give perfective искључити; the required imperfective is искључивати, which covers disconnecting an appliance from the socket. / Both answers use perfective искључити, which breaks the imperfective rule. Искључивати covers unplugging an appliance from the socket. · judge: fail: искључивати alone means switching off or disconnecting generally, not specifically pulling a plug from a socket; the sense is lost without a phrase. |
| `monotonous_adj` | monotonous (dull and repetitive without change) | монотон | једнообразан | a / a → монотон | ok | Монотон means dull and repetitive, fitting a boring job; једнообразан means uniform and lacks the tedium. / Монотон is the common adjective for dull, unchanging work; једнообразан means uniform and lacks the boring nuance. |
| `prohibitive_adj` | prohibitive (so high it prevents purchase or use) | превисок | неприступачан | a / a → превисок | ok | A prohibitive cost is one too high to pay; превисок says exactly that, while неприступачан means inaccessible. / Превисок says the cost is too high for most to pay; неприступачан means inaccessible, a different sense. |
| `profess_v` | profess (claim to have a belief or feeling) | изјављивати | исповедати | a / a → изјављивати | ok | Professing ignorance is a declaration; imperfective изјављивати fits, whereas исповедати relates to faith or confession and suits belief rather than ignorance. / Imperfective verb for declaring or claiming something, as in claiming ignorance of rules; исповедати suggests confessing or religious faith. |
| `devotee_n` | devotee (person who greatly admires something) | поштовалац | поклоник | b / b → поклоник | ok | Поклоник is the common word for an ardent admirer of an art; поштовалац is weaker, merely a respecter. / Поклоник is the usual word for an ardent admirer of poetry or art; поштовалац is formal respect. |
| `facilitation_n` | facilitation (making a process easier or smoother) | олакшица | омогућавање | b / other → tie: first → олакшавање | ok | Омогућавање is an established verbal noun; олакшица is not a dictionary word for a process and means a concession. / Олакшица means a concession and омогућавање means enabling; олакшавање is the verbal noun of олакшавати, to make easier. → tie: Олакшавање is the verbal noun of олакшавати, to make easier, matching the sense; омогућавање means enabling and олакшица a concession. · judge: pass: Олакшавање is the regular verbal noun of олакшавати, an established word meaning making something easier. |
| `tantamount_adj` | tantamount (equal in seriousness to something bad) | равно | једнак | other / other → tie: first → равносилан | ok | Равно is an adverb and једнак is merely 'equal'; равносилан is the established adjective for 'equivalent in effect', fitting silence equalling admission. / Admission example needs the established adjective meaning equivalent to; равно is an adverb and једнак is plain equal. → tie: Равносилан means equivalent in effect, as silence equal to admission; равно is an adverb and једнак merely says equal. · judge: pass: Равносилан (равносилан признању) is an established adjective meaning equivalent in effect; fits the example. |
| `impersonal_adj` | impersonal (lacking human warmth or emotion) | безличан | неособан | a / a → безличан | ok | Безличан is the established word for a cold, characterless place; неособан is not a standard dictionary adjective. / Безличан is the established word for cold, characterless places; неособан is not a normal dictionary word in this sense. |
| `battered_adj` | battered (damaged by repeated blows or use) | издрндан | оштећен | other / other → излупан | ok | Battered old car means knocked about by blows or use; излупан is the common adjective, оштећен is too generic and издрндан slangy. / Издрндан is slangy and rare, оштећен is too generic; излупан is the common word for a battered old car. · judge: pass: излупан is an established colloquial adjective for something knocked about, as in излупан ауто, fitting the old car. |
| `confide_v` | confide (tell secrets to a friend) | поверити | поведавати | other / other → поверавати | ok | A is perfective and поведавати is not a standard word; the imperfective поверавати is the established verb for confiding fears. / Поверити is perfective; imperfective поверавати means to entrust secrets or fears to someone, as in the example. · judge: pass: поверавати is the imperfective verb for confiding or entrusting secrets and fears, perfective поверити. |

Миран дан is the normal way to say a day with nothing happening. · judge: pass: миран дан is natural Serbian for a quiet uneventful day; миран is common and fits the example. |
| `dissect_v` | dissect (cut up for study) | сецирати | дисектовати | a / a → сецирати | ok | Сецирати is the established verb for dissecting a body or animal; the -ирати verb works as infinitive, while дисектовати is nonstandard. / The native anatomical verb for dissecting a frog; дисектовати is a flagged, less usual borrowing. |
| `daunting_adj` | daunting (seeming difficult or frightening to deal with) | застрашујући | заплашујући | a / a → застрашујући | ok | Застрашујући is the common adjective for an intimidating task; заплашујући is rarer and leans toward deliberate threatening. / Common adjective for a task that seems frightening or overwhelming; заплашујући implies deliberate intimidation. |
| `startling_adj` | startling (very surprising or shocking) | запрепашћујући | изненађујући | a / b → tie: second → изненађујући | ok | The sense includes shocking news, which запрепашћујући captures; изненађујући only conveys mild surprise. / Common neutral adjective for surprising news; запрепашћујући is rarer and heavier, more like appalling. → tie: Common neutral adjective for surprising news; запрепашћујући is rarer, heavier and nearer appalling than startling. |
| `telltale_adj` | telltale (showing something hidden) | разоткривајући | окарактерисан | unsure / other → tie: first → — | quarantined | Разоткривајући is a participle built for the occasion and окарактерисан means characterized; I know no established single adjective for telltale in this meaning. / A telltale sign of rain is a revealing sign; речит (as in речит знак) is established, while разоткривајући is a participle and окарактерисан means characterized. → tie: Речит знак, a sign that speaks for itself, matches a telltale sign of rain; разоткривајући is an ad hoc participle. · judge: pass: Речит as in речит знак or доказ means telling and revealing; acceptable for a telltale sign. · term override: речит means eloquent; Serbian has no established single adjective for telltale (a telltale sign is a description: знак који одаје) |

| `gush_v` | gush (flow out suddenly and in large amounts) | јурити | избијати | other / other → шикљати | ok | Blood gushing from a wound is шикљати, imperfective and vivid; јурити means to run or chase, and избијати suits eruptions. / Јурити means to chase or rush and избијати means to break out; шикљати is the established imperfective for blood spurting from a wound. · judge: pass: Шикљати is an imperfective verb for liquid bursting out in a jet, and крв шикља из ране matches the example. |

| `unmanageable_adj` | unmanageable (difficult to control or deal with) | неуправљив | неконтролисан | a / a → неуправљив | ok | A situation that cannot be handled is неуправљив; неконтролисан means uncontrolled or unchecked, describing a state rather than difficulty. / Неуправљив covers things impossible to steer or handle, fitting a situation getting out of hand; неконтролисан means merely uncontrolled. |
| `quip_n` | quip (a clever funny remark) | доскочица | досетка | b / b → досетка | ok | Досетка is the common established noun for a witty remark; доскочица is much rarer. / Досетка is the established everyday word for a witty remark; доскочица is a rare bookish word. |
| `quip_v` | quip (make a clever funny remark) | нашалити се | рећи | other / other → шалити се | collision | Нашалити се is perfective and рећи is too generic; шалити се is the imperfective established verb for joking. / Both options are perfective and нашалити се means to have had one's fill of joking; the imperfective шалити се fits saying something wittily. · judge: pass: шалити се is the established imperfective verb for joking, and 'нашалила се да је уморна' fits the quipped example. |
| `zany_adj` | zany (amusingly unusual or eccentric) | луд | бизаран | other / other → шашав | ok | A zany costume is funnily eccentric; луд means crazy and бизаран bizarre, while шашав is the established adjective for goofy, comically odd. / Шашав means goofy and wacky in a funny way; луд is plain crazy and бизаран is bizarre without the humour. · judge: pass: шашав is an established colloquial adjective for goofy, eccentric, amusingly odd, masculine nominative. |
| `brainwash_v` | brainwash (force someone to change beliefs) | испрати мозак | мозгати | other / other → tie: second → испирати мозак | ok | A is perfective (испрати); the imperfective idiom испирати мозак is the established verb phrase, while мозгати is not an established word. / Испрати мозак is perfective and мозгати is not a real word; the imperfective idiom прати мозак means to brainwash. → tie: Imperfective idiom испирати мозак is the established Serbian expression for brainwashing; мозгати is not a real word. · judge: pass: Испирати мозак is the standard fixed expression, in imperfective form, for brainwashing. |

| `clench_v` | clench (to close tightly) | стезати | стиснути | other / other → стискати | collision | Clenching fists is стискати песнице; стиснути is perfective and стезати suggests tightening or clamping rather than closing a fist. / Стиснути is perfective, and стезати means tighten; стискати is the imperfective used for clenching fists or teeth. · judge: pass: стискати is the common imperfective verb for tightening, as in стискати песнице for clenched fists. |

| `dutiful_adj` | dutiful (doing what is expected) | послушан | покоран | a / other → tie: other → савестан | collision | A dutiful student does what is expected; послушан suits this, while покоран means submissive, with a servile tone. / A student doing all homework is conscientious; послушан and покоран mean obedient or submissive, a different sense. → tie: A student finishing all homework is conscientious; послушан and покоран mean obedient or submissive, a different sense. · judge: pass: савестан is an established adjective for conscientious, duty-fulfilling, and fits the diligent student. |

| `refurbish_v` | refurbish (renovate or redecorate a building) | обнављати | обновити | other / a → tie: other → реновирати | ok | Renovating a hotel is exactly реновирати; обнављати is vaguer (restore, renew) and обновити is perfective. / Imperfective required, so a is right and b is its perfective partner; обнављати хотел covers renovating a building. → tie: Renovating a hotel is exactly реновирати, a biaspectual -ирати verb; обнављати is vaguer and обновити is perfective. · judge: pass: реновирати is an established, common biaspectual verb for renovating a building, usable as imperfective. |

| `appal_v` | appal (shock or horrify greatly) | запрепастити | шокирати | other / other → ужасавати | ok | Both answers are perfective or merely mild (shock); imperfective ужасавати is the established verb for horrifying greatly, matching cruelty that appals. / Both answers are perfective; the imperfective ужасавати (partner ужаснути) conveys horrifying the public with cruelty, stronger than plain shock. · judge: pass: ужасавати is the established imperfective verb for horrifying, fitting cruelty appalling the public. |
| `transfix_v` | transfix (cause to become motionless with shock) | заледити | приковати | unsure / other → tie: first → — | quarantined | Both answers are perfective and mean freezing or nailing in place; I know no established imperfective single verb for being transfixed by a speech. / Заледити and приковати are perfective and off-meaning; парализовати is used for both aspects and means to freeze someone with shock. → tie: Парализовати is biaspectual and means freezing someone motionless with shock; заледити and приковати are perfective and off in sense. · judge: fail: Парализовати is flagged as perfective and means to paralyze, not to hold someone motionless in shock or fascination, so aspect and sense are off. |
| `exhilarate_v` | exhilarate (make someone very happy and energetic) | развеселити | бодрити | unsure / b → tie: first → бодрити | ok | развеселити is perfective and only cheers up; бодрити means to encourage; no established imperfective verb clearly means making someone thrilled and energetic. / Imperfective and conveys making someone energetic and lively, as fresh air does; развеселити is perfective and means only cheering up. → tie: Imperfective бодрити conveys invigorating someone, as fresh air does; развеселити is perfective and only cheers up. |
| `digitalise_v` | digitalise (convert information to digital format) | дигитализовати | дигитализовати | a / a → — | quarantined | Verbs in -овати are biaspectual in Serbian, so дигитализовати serves as the imperfective; no other established verb exists. / Established -овати loan verb, used biaspectually in Serbian, so the perfective flag is moot; both answers identical. |
| `aptitude_n` | aptitude (natural ability to learn something) | способност | таленат | a / b → tie: second → способност | collision | Способност за језике is the natural collocation; most frequent Wiktionary candidate. Таленат implies exceptional gift and is a loan. / Таленат names innate ability for something like languages; способност is any ability, learned or not. → tie: Способност за језике is the natural collocation and most frequent; таленат is a loan implying exceptional gift. |
| `unadulterated_adj` | unadulterated (pure and not mixed with anything else) | чист | непримешан | a / a → чист | collision | Чист is the common neutral word for pure air; непримешан is bookish and mostly used of substances. / Чист is the common neutral adjective for pure air; непримешан is bookish and rarer for describing air. |
