## Quarantined (never shown)

| key | English (sense) | best guess | reasons |
|---|---|---|---|
| `debut_v` | debut (to make a first public appearance) | дебитовати | perfective verb (DeepSeek and local model) · adjudicator: Дебитовати is a biaspectual established verb for a first public appearance, so it is acceptable as the infinitive for 'debuted'. / Both answers identical; дебитовати is the established verb for a first public appearance and works in both aspects. |
| `pace_v` | pace (walk back and forth nervously) | корачати | judge_other: корачати means to stride or step, not to walk back and forth nervously; the sense calls for шетати горе-доле. · adjudicator: Корачати по соби is the established verb for pacing; ходати is just walking and шетајти is a malformed Ijekavian-like non-word. / шетајти is a misspelling and means stroll; ходати тамо-амо fits pacing a room while waiting. → tie: Корачати по соби is the established verb for pacing a room; ходати is plain walking and шетајти is a non-word. |
| `humble_v` | humble (to make someone feel less important) | понижавати | judge_other: понижавати means to humiliate or degrade, which is stronger and more hostile than humble, as the back-translation itself shows. · adjudicator: Понизити is perfective and свестранити is invented; imperfective понижавати is the established verb for bringing someone low, as defeat did to the champion. / Понизити is perfective and свестранити is not a word. Понижавати, imperfective, covers lowering a proud champion after defeat. |
| `premises_n` | premises (land and buildings owned by an organization) | просторије | Ijekavian form; the Ekavian просторе is as common · adjudicator: Простор is general space, and b is Ijekavian-flagged; the plural-only просторије is the established word for premises of an organization. / Простор means space; the plural-only просторије is the established word for an organization's buildings and grounds, in nominative plural. |
| `pioneer_v` | pioneer (to develop or introduce something new) | уводити | judge_other: уводити only means to bring in or introduce; it lacks the sense of being first to develop something new, so it is too generic for pioneer. · adjudicator: Пионирити and пионирисати are coined from the noun, not established dictionary verbs; Serbian expresses this only with phrases like 'бити пионир у'. / Пионирити and пионирисати are not established Serbian verbs and are perfective-flagged coinages. Уводити (нову методу) is the dictionary verb for introducing something new. → tie: Пионирити and пионирисати are coinages; уводити нову методу is the established imperfective verb for introducing something new. |
| `scrutiny_n` | scrutiny (close and careful examination) | провера | judge_other: провера means a check or verification, a weaker and different meaning than close, careful scrutiny of a plan. · adjudicator: Close examination of a plan; a's преглед is a plain check or review, lacking the careful intensity of scrutiny. / A plan under close scrutiny is checked thoroughly; преглед is a routine inspection and b is a two-word gloss. → tie: A plan under close scrutiny is thoroughly checked; провера is one established noun, whereas преглед is routine inspection. |
| `induction_n` | induction (formal introduction to a job or organization) | увођење | judge_other: Bare увођење only means generic introduction or leading in; the job-induction sense needs the phrase увођење у посао, so the single term is not exact. · adjudicator: Staff induction is introducing new employees to work, standardly увођење у посао; увод means a preface, упознавање just meeting. / Увод is a preface and упознавање only acquaintance; увођење (у посао) is the established noun for formal introduction of new staff. |
| `fixture_n` | fixture (permanent object attached to a building) | уређај | adjudicator unsure · adjudicator: Уређај means appliance and фиксни део is a calque; I know no established single Serbian noun for a building fixture like a light fitting. / Serbian has no single established word for a building fixture; уређај means appliance and фиксни део is not an established term. |
| `flick_v` | flick (strike with a quick movement) | стресати | judge_other: Стресати means to shake off, not to strike with a quick movement, and the gloss and back-translation do not match the headword sense. · adjudicator: Flicking dust off a coat is отресати, the imperfective of отрести, which both answers wrongly give perfective or as slapping. / Both answers are perfective and mean shake or slap; стресати is the imperfective used for flicking dust off clothing. → tie: Imperfective стресати is the usual verb for flicking dust or crumbs off clothing; both given verbs were perfective. |
| `flourish_n` | flourish (bold or dramatic gesture) | запетљај | adjudicator unsure · adjudicator: Замахе means swings and запетљај is doubtful; I am not confident of an established word for a dramatic flourish. / No established single Serbian noun fits a flourish added to a signature; запетљај means a tangle and замахе is not a word. |
| `numb_adj` | numb (unable to feel sensation) | утрнуо | judge_other: Утрнуо is the past participle of the perfective verb утрнути, not a dictionary adjective; a form such as утрнут or укочен would be needed. |
| `numb_v` | numb (make unable to feel sensation) | умртвљивати | judge_other: умртвљивати is a rare bookish derived imperfective, not the common word for numbing pain; learners would meet анестезирати or утрнути instead. · adjudicator: Укочити is perfective and means stiffen, not deaden feeling; анестезирати is established, biaspectual, and means to make a body part insensible. / Укочити is perfective and means to stiffen, not to deaden feeling; умртвљивати is imperfective and used for deadening pain or sensation. → tie: Imperfective умртвљивати is used for deadening pain or sensation; укочити is perfective and means stiffen. |
| `plunge_v` | plunge (to fall or drop quickly and deeply) | падати | judge_other: падати is plain falling and loses the sudden, steep, deep drop of plunge; a diving plane needs a more specific verb than generic падати. · adjudicator: Both answers are perfective пасти; the imperfective падати is required and covers a plane beginning to drop towards the ground. / Both answers give perfective пасти; the imperfective падати fits a plane falling quickly towards the ground. |
| `divergent_adj` | divergent (moving or growing apart) | дивергентан | judge_other: дивергентан is a jargon calque of the English word; Serbian uses различит or који се разилази for diverging paths. · adjudicator: Различит only means different and разилазећи is a participle, not a listed adjective; no established word clearly means moving apart. / Real adjective fitting friends' differing paths in life; b's разилазећи is a participle, not a dictionary adjective. → tie: Различит only means different; разилазећи is a participle; дивергентан is the established adjective for diverging. |
| `resent_v` | resent (feel angry about unfair treatment) | замерати | judge_other: замерати means to hold something against someone or blame them, which differs from resenting unfair treatment; the gloss also contains a non-Serbian form осећать. · adjudicator: огорчавати means to embitter someone and завадити се means quarrel; негодовати is the imperfective for feeling indignant at unfair treatment. / Огорчавати is transitive 'embitter' and завадити се is perfective 'quarrel'; замерати means to resent or hold something against someone. → tie: Imperfective замерати means resenting or holding something against someone; огорчавати is transitive embitter and завадити се perfective quarrel. |
| `wrench_v` | wrench (twist or pull suddenly) | трзати | judge_other: трзати means to jerk, twitch or yank repeatedly, not a single forceful wrench of a door open; the back-translation tug is weaker than the sense. · adjudicator: Both answers are perfective or off-sense; the imperfective трзати expresses a sudden jerking pull, as with a door. / Тргнути and окренути are perfective; imperfective трзати covers a sudden violent pull, as when wrenching a stuck door. |
| `forfeit_v` | forfeit (lose something as a penalty) | губити | judge_other: губити only means to lose and carries no penalty meaning; forfeiting a match is predati or izgubiti bez borbe, so the sense is lost. · adjudicator: Both answers are perfective изгубити; the imperfective губити is the established verb, and the penalty meaning comes from context like losing a match. / Изгубити is perfective and flagged; imperfective губити, as in losing a match or right by penalty, is allowed and fits. |
| `dismay_v` | dismay (to make someone feel shocked and sad) | запрепашћивати | judge_other: запрепашћивати is a rare bookish imperfective meaning to stun; not a common word a learner should get for dismay. · adjudicator: А is perfective запрепастити; the imperfective запрепашћивати fits bad news shocking everyone, and депримирати means depress, not shock. / Both answers are perfective or wrong sense; the imperfective запрепашћивати is the proper form meaning to dismay someone. |
| `astray_adv` | astray (away from the correct path or direction) | залутало | adjudicator unsure · adjudicator: Залутало is a participle form and скретајући a gerund; neither is an established adverb, and no clear single Serbian adverb exists. / Залутало is a neuter participle and скретајући a present participle, neither an adverb; Serbian has no established single adverb for astray, only phrases like на странпутици. |
| `conjure_v` | conjure (make something appear magically) | чарати | override: чарати means to charm or bewitch, not to make appear by magic; Serbian has no established single verb for conjure |
| `tangle_n` | tangle (messy knot of strings or wires) | запетљај | adjudicator unsure · adjudicator: Запетљај and запутаница are not clearly established nouns for a tangle of wires; Serbian has no secure single standard word here. / запетљај and запутаница are not clearly listed for a tangle of wires; no confident established single noun, so unsure. |
| `pounce_v` | pounce (jump or move suddenly and quickly) | насртати | judge_other: насртати means to attack or assail someone, not to jump suddenly; the gloss contradicts the term, and the sense is the leap itself. · adjudicator: Both answers give perfective скочити; the imperfective скакати is the required form, and it covers a cat leaping on a mouse. / Скочити is perfective and plain jumping; насртати is imperfective and means to lunge at suddenly, as a cat on a mouse. → tie: Imperfective насртати means lunging suddenly at prey, as a cat on a mouse; скочити is perfective plain jumping. |
| `unplug_v` | unplug (disconnect from an electrical socket) | искључивати | judge_other: искључивати alone means switching off or disconnecting generally, not specifically pulling a plug from a socket; the sense is lost without a phrase. · adjudicator: Both answers give perfective искључити; the required imperfective is искључивати, which covers disconnecting an appliance from the socket. / Both answers use perfective искључити, which breaks the imperfective rule. Искључивати covers unplugging an appliance from the socket. |
| `telltale_adj` | telltale (showing something hidden) | речит | override: речит means eloquent; Serbian has no established single adjective for telltale (a telltale sign is a description: знак који одаје) · adjudicator: Разоткривајући is a participle built for the occasion and окарактерисан means characterized; I know no established single adjective for telltale in this meaning. / A telltale sign of rain is a revealing sign; речит (as in речит знак) is established, while разоткривајући is a participle and окарактерисан means characterized. → tie: Речит знак, a sign that speaks for itself, matches a telltale sign of rain; разоткривајући is an ad hoc participle. |
| `plummet_v` | plummet (to fall or drop straight down at high speed) | падати | judge_other: падати only means to fall and drops the sudden steep plunge of plummet; the sense needs a verb like стрмоглављивати се, so the plain verb is too weak. · adjudicator: Answer b's пасти is perfective, so its imperfective падати is the term; a's обрушивати се is a misspelt form meaning to collapse onto something, not plunge. / Answer b пасти is perfective and a обрушивати се means collapse or crash onto; падати is the common imperfective for falling down, matching the Russian partner. |
| `dislodge_v` | dislodge (force something out of a position) | избацивати | judge_other: избацивати means to throw or eject out, usually deliberately; it does not convey forcing a stone loose from its position. · adjudicator: Both answers are perfective; the imperfective избацивати fits water forcing a stone out of its place. / Both are perfective; the imperfective избацивати fits water forcing a stone out of its position. |
| `sketchbook_n` | sketchbook (notebook for drawing sketches) | скицбук | judge_other: скицбук is an English loanword used informally, not an established dictionary word; Serbian uses блок за скицирање or скицирка. · adjudicator: Скицбокс is a coined hybrid and скулпторски блок means sculptor's block; I know no settled single Serbian word. / Скулпторски блок means sculptor's block, a wrong sense; скицбокс is a distorted spelling, while скицбук is the loanword in use. → tie: Скулпторски блок is a wrong sense and скицбокс a garbled spelling; скицбук is the loanword actually used. |
| `scrutinise_v` | scrutinise (examine something very carefully) | проверавати | judge_other: проверавати means to check or verify, not to examine very closely; it drops the intense, careful inspection of scrutinise. · adjudicator: Checking a document for errors; a's phrase is perfective, b's 'позорно' is not idiomatic, so imperfective проверавати fits. / A is perfective and позорно испитивати is not natural Serbian; проучавати is an imperfective single word for examining closely. → tie: Checking a document for errors is проверавати, imperfective; the given phrases are perfective or unidiomatic (позорно). |
| `cleverness_n` | cleverness (quality of being intelligent and quick) | довитљивост | judge_other: Довитљивост means resourcefulness or ready wit, not general intelligence and quickness; its gloss differs, and памет or бистрина is the more common fit. · adjudicator: Quick intelligence solving a puzzle is памет as wit; спретност means dexterity or skill with hands, the wrong sense. / Памет also means brains or intelligence, fitting his cleverness; спретност means dexterity or skill, a different quality. |
| `fiddly_adj` | fiddly (requiring careful small movements) | мукотрпан | judge_other: Мукотрпан means arduous or toilsome, a hard laborious effort, not delicate work needing small precise movements, so the sense is wrong. · adjudicator: Ситничав describes a job full of tiny details needing precision, like watch repair; захтеван means demanding in general, not fine handwork. / Захтеван is a real adjective for a task needing care; ситничав describes a petty, fussy person, not a delicate watch repair. → tie: Watch repair full of tiny details fits ситничав (fine, finicky work); захтеван only means demanding in general. |
| `exhibitionist_n` | exhibitionist (person who shows off publicly) | егзибиционист | judge_other: егзибиционист in Serbian chiefly means a person who indecently exposes themselves, not a general show-off, so it misses the politician sense. · adjudicator: Both end in -ониста, a non-Serbian feminine-looking form; the established masculine noun is егзибиционист, also used for show-offs. / Both answers end in -ста, wrongly feminine-looking for a masculine noun; the established Serbian form is егзибиционист, also used for a show-off. |
| `transfix_v` | transfix (cause to become motionless with shock) | парализовати | perfective verb (local model); judge_other: Парализовати is flagged as perfective and means to paralyze, not to hold someone motionless in shock or fascination, so aspect and sense are off. · adjudicator: Both answers are perfective and mean freezing or nailing in place; I know no established imperfective single verb for being transfixed by a speech. / Заледити and приковати are perfective and off-meaning; парализовати is used for both aspects and means to freeze someone with shock. → tie: Парализовати is biaspectual and means freezing someone motionless with shock; заледити and приковати are perfective and off in sense. |
| `digitalise_v` | digitalise (convert information to digital format) | дигитализовати | perfective verb (DeepSeek and local model) · adjudicator: Verbs in -овати are biaspectual in Serbian, so дигитализовати serves as the imperfective; no other established verb exists. / Established -овати loan verb, used biaspectually in Serbian, so the perfective flag is moot; both answers identical. |

## Duplicate synonyms in Serbian (never shown)

Another meaning has the same term in this language and no hint tells the two apart, so only that one keeps it. Not a translation failure.

| key | English (sense) | term | kept by |
|---|---|---|---|
| `collaboration_n` | collaboration (working together on a project) | сарадња | `cooperation_n` (working together to achieve a goal) |
| `clutch_v` | clutch (to hold something tightly with your hand) | стискати | `grip_v` (to hold something tightly) |
| `appalling_adj` | appalling (very bad or shocking) | ужасан | `terrible_adj` (very bad or unpleasant) |
| `irate_adj` | irate (very angry) | бесан | `furious_adj` (very angry) |

## Review: wrong and doubtful

| key | term | status | review |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mark_up_n` | маржа | ok | doubtful: Маржа suits trade margin, but the hint 'додавана вредност' means value added, a different concept from mark-up. (better: маржа) |
| `stock_v` | снабдевати | collision | doubtful: Снабдевати means to supply someone; a shop stocking bread is better rendered as држати у понуди. (better: држати у понуди) |
| `jersey_n` | мајица | collision | doubtful: Мајица is a T-shirt; a sports jersey is normally дрес in Serbian, so the term misses the sports sense. (better: дрес) |
| `premier_adj` | главни | collision | doubtful: Главни means main in general; водећи or први better conveys the first rank of importance in premier reason. (better: водећи) |
| `coverage_n` | извештавање | ok | doubtful: Извештавање is right for media coverage, but the hint 'покривање догађаја' is a calque; 'праћење у медијима' is more natural. (better: извештавање) |
| `debut_n` | деби | ok | doubtful: Деби is mostly sports-media slang; the standard rendering of a first public appearance is први наступ or дебитовање. (better: први наступ) |
| `recall_n` | сећање | ok | doubtful: Сећање is a single memory or act of remembering; the ability to recall is памћење. (better: памћење) |
| `diversity_n` | разноликост | ok | doubtful: Разноликост exists but разноврсност is the commoner word for diversity; hint itself uses разноврсност. (better: разноликост) |
| `criteria_n` | критеријум | ok | doubtful: Headword is plural 'criteria' but the term is singular критеријум; the plural критеријуми matches the example 'criteria are strict'. (better: критеријуми) |
| `scenario_n` | сценарио | ok | wrong: Standard Serbian form is сценарио; сценариј is the Croatian scenarij, breaking the Serbian-standard convention. (better: сценарио) |
| `chill_n` | хладноћа | ok | doubtful: A slight chill or shiver of coldness is језа or студ; хладноћа is just generic coldness. (better: језа) |
| `structural_adj` | структурални | ok | doubtful: Структурални is a definite form, not the dictionary form; the common adjective is структурни. (better: структурни) |
| `burst_n` | налет | ok | doubtful: Налет (налет енергије) is natural, but the hint uses нагон, which means instinct, not a surge of energy. (better: налет) |
| `self_conscious_adj` | несигуран | collision | doubtful: Несигуран means insecure or unsure, not specifically awkwardly aware of being observed; стидљив or постидан is closer. (better: стидљив) |
| `restoration_n` | обнова | ok | doubtful: Обнова means renewal or rebuilding; restoring a painting is normally рестаурација. (better: рестаурација) |
| `facial_adj` | фацијални | ok | doubtful: Фацијални is a medical Latinism; everyday Serbian uses a genitive phrase like повреда лица, no common adjective. (better: повреда лица) |
| `facial_n` | третман лица | ok | doubtful: Term is fine, but note 'g: m' is redundant because a consonant-final noun already shows masculine gender. (better: третман лица) |
| `lodge_v` | одседати | ok | doubtful: Одседати suggests staying at a hotel or inn, not with friends, and the note lacks perfective partner одсести. (better: боравити) |
| `ethical_adj` | етички | ok | doubtful: Етички means relating to ethics; 'Is it ethical to...' (morally acceptable) is normally етичан. (better: етичан) |
| `hostile_adj` | непријатељски | ok | doubtful: Term is fine, but hint 'непријазан' is not a standard Serbian word; should be непријатељски настројен or непријатан. (better: непријатељски) |
| `elaborate_adj` | разрађен | ok | doubtful: Разрађен means worked-out or developed; for elaborate costumes сложен or китњаст is more natural. (better: сложен) |
| `elaborate_v` | појашњавати | ok | doubtful: Појашњавати means to clarify; 'elaborate on a point' is closer to разрађивати, and the pf partner is missing from the note. (better: разрађивати) |
| `hack_n` | трик | collision | doubtful: Трик is understandable but the hint 'корисна вештина' (useful skill) is inexact; лајфхак is what Serbian speakers say for life hack. (better: лајфхак) |
| `hack_v` | сећи | ok | doubtful: Сећи is generic cutting and loses the rough, chopping sense; сецкати or кресати fits hacking through bushes better. (better: сецкати) |
| `harvest_v` | жети | ok | doubtful: Жети mainly means reaping grain; убирати covers crops generally, and no perfective note is given. (better: убирати) |
| `sentiment_n` | осећање | ok | doubtful: Расположење normally means mood; for public opinion or feeling осећање or став fits sentiment better. (better: осећање) |
| `sequel_n` | наставак | ok | doubtful: Term наставак is right, but hint 'наставни део' is wrong: наставни means educational; use 'наставак приче или филма'. (better: наставак) |
| `inclusive_adj` | укључен | collision | doubtful: Укључен mostly means switched on or included as a participle; for 'inclusive of tax' it is stiff, and the hint is clumsy. (better: свеобухватан) |
| `integral_adj` | саставни | ok | doubtful: Саставни means constituent (саставни део); trust being integral to a relationship is closer to неизоставан or битан. (better: неизоставан) |
| `swap_v` | мењати | collision | doubtful: Мењати is too broad and its pf is променити or заменити; for swapping seats, замењивати with pf заменити is more precise. (better: замењивати) |
| `demographic_adj` | демографски | ok | doubtful: Term is correct, but the hint misspells становништво as 'становниство', which would teach a wrong spelling. (better: демографски) |
| `demographic_n` | демографска карактеристика | ok | doubtful: Two-word coinage that mirrors the English; the noun sense is better covered by демографски показатељ or демографска група. (better: демографски показатељ) |
| `woo_v` | удварати се | ok | doubtful: Удварати се is right and imperfective, but the hint 'тежити љубави некога' is unnatural Serbian; 'придобијати наклоност' would be clearer. |
| `obscure_adj` | непознат | collision | doubtful: Непознат means unknown or stranger; obscure meaning little known is better rendered малопознат. (better: малопознат) |
| `atmospheric_adj` | пун атмосфере | ok | wrong: Атмосферски means relating to the Earth's atmosphere, not mood-creating; candlelight being atmospheric needs a phrase like пун атмосфере. (better: пун атмосфере) |
| `strap_v` | причвршћивати | ok | doubtful: Причвршћивати means fixing in general; the hint uses archaic 'појасити', and no pf is noted (причврстити); везивати fits strapping a suitcase. (better: везивати) |
| `buffer_n` | амортизер | ok | doubtful: Амортизер is a shock absorber in a vehicle; for a general buffer, as in a cushion or buffer zone, тампон fits better. (better: тампон) |
| `collaborative_adj` | сараднички | ok | doubtful: Сараднички is rare and sounds bureaucratic; заједнички or кооперативан is more natural for a collaborative approach. (better: заједнички) |
| `render_v` | чинити | collision | doubtful: Чинити is acceptable imperfective, but this sense is normally expressed by учинити, and the note lacks a perfective partner. |
| `prop_v` | подупирати | ok | doubtful: Подупирати is right and imperfective, but the note omits the perfective partner подупрети. |
| `occupational_adj` | професионални | ok | doubtful: Професионални is the definite form, breaking the indefinite dictionary convention; also professional can mislead, though професионални ризик is used. (better: професионалан) |
| `tract_n` | пространство | ok | doubtful: Пространство is vague and means space or expanse; for a tract of land, површина земље or парцела is more precise. (better: површина) |
| `apprentice_n` | шегрт | ok | doubtful: Шегрт is right, but the hint contains Russian ё (учёник); it must be ученик у занату. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: особа која учи занат) |
| `durable_adj` | издржљив | ok | doubtful: Издржљив mostly describes people's stamina; for durable shoes or goods, трајан or отпоран is more natural. (better: трајан) |
| `hypocrisy_n` | лицемерје | ok | doubtful: Term лицемерје is right, but hint 'несрдачност' means lack of cordiality, not hypocrisy; better hint 'претварање, неискреност'. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: претварање, неискреност) |
| `salvage_v` | спасавати | collision | doubtful: Спасавати is acceptable, but the note lacks the perfective partner спасити that the convention expects for verbs. |
| `plausible_adj` | вероватан | collision | doubtful: Вероватан means probable; plausible (sounding convincing) is better rendered by уверљив or веродостојан. (better: уверљив) |
| `comprise_v` | састојати се | collision | doubtful: Састојати се је the right verb, but the hint 'чинити целину' means to form a whole, the reverse direction. |
| `reminiscent_adj` | подсећајући | ok | doubtful: Подсећајући is a stiff calque; Serbian prefers the clause који подсећа на, and the pleasant sense is missing. (better: који подсећа на) |
| `conditional_adj` | услован | ok | doubtful: The dictionary masculine form has a fleeting a, услован; услован is not the standard form. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept) |
| `plumbing_n` | водовод | ok | doubtful: Водовод means the water supply network; the pipes and fittings in a building are водоинсталације or водоводне инсталације. (better: водоинсталације) |
| `liaison_n` | веза | collision | doubtful: Веза alone is ambiguous (relationship, connection); for a person liaison use особа за везу or контакт-особа. (better: особа за везу) |
| `rotate_v` | окретати се | collision | doubtful: Окретати се is fine, but the hint uses non-existent вртелити; it should be вртети се око осе. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: вртети се око осе) |
| `victorious_adj` | победнички | ok | doubtful: Победнички means winner's or champion-like; победоносан is the usual adjective for victorious, though the team usage is acceptable. (better: победоносан) |
| `batter_v` | ударати | collision | doubtful: Ударати is a plain 'hit' and loses the violent, repeated pounding of waves battering a coast; тући conveys it better. (better: тући) |
| `waterfront_n` | обала | collision | doubtful: Обала means shore or bank generally; приобаље or кеј better convey the built-up waterfront area people walk along. (better: приобаље) |
| `portrayal_n` | приказивање | ok | doubtful: For an actor's portrayal of a role, тумачење улоге is more natural than приказивање. (better: тумачење) |
| `conform_v` | прилагођавати се | ok | doubtful: Прилагођавати се means to adapt; conforming to rules is better rendered by повиновати се or придржавати се. (better: повиновати се) |
| `flourish_v` | напредовати | collision | doubtful: Напредовати means to progress or advance; for a garden thriving, успевати or цветати fits better than the hint успешно расти. (better: успевати) |
| `heroine_n` | јунакиња | ok | doubtful: Term is right, but the hint 'главна женска лик' has wrong gender agreement; лик is masculine, so 'главни женски лик'. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: главни женски лик) |
| `manoeuvre_n` | маневар | ok | doubtful: Маневар is right, but the hint вешта покрет has a gender agreement error; it should be вешт покрет. |
| `numb_adj` | утрнуо | quarantined | wrong: Укочен means stiff or rigid, not lacking sensation; fingers numb from cold are утрнули, so term mismatches the hint. (better: утрнуо) |
| `quirky_adj` | необичан | ok | doubtful: Необичан is merely unusual; чудноват or ексцентричан captures the quirky charm better. (better: чудноват) |
| `reap_v` | жети | collision | doubtful: The perfective of жети is пожњети, not 'пожети'; the note is incorrect, though the figurative use is fine. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; pf пожњети not confirmed; pf пожети kept (DeepSeek gives it, or wasn't asked)) |
| `chilly_adj` | хладан | collision | doubtful: Хладан is plain cold; chilly, unpleasantly cool, is better matched by прохладан, a more exact fit. (better: прохладан) |
| `lapse_v` | западати | ok | doubtful: Западати is awkward with 'into silence'; 'западати у сан' exists but тонути у тишину is more natural. (better: тонути) |
| `graft_v` | радити | collision | doubtful: Plain радити lacks the sense of gruelling effort; the hint carries the meaning but the term itself is too generic. (better: вредно радити) |
| `revealing_adj` | речит | ok | doubtful: Речит means eloquent or telling and can fit an expression, but it is not a clear match for 'revealing hidden information'. (better: речит) |
| `confinement_n` | заточеништво | ok | wrong: Затвор means prison, not the abstract state of being confined; hint itself says заточеништво, which should be the term. (better: заточеништво) |
| `suffice_v` | достајати | ok | doubtful: Достајати is rare and bookish; learners will normally meet бити довољан or the impersonal довољно је. (better: бити довољан) |
| `gadget_n` | геџет | ok | doubtful: Геџет is a rare phonetic loanword; направа, справа or уређај are the more common Serbian words for a gadget. (better: направа) |
| `deprivation_n` | лишавање | ok | doubtful: Лишавање is the act of depriving; deprivation as lack of necessities is better оскудица or лишеност. (better: оскудица) |
| `bouquet_n` | букет | ok | doubtful: Букет is right, but the hint 'скуп исечених цвећа' is ungrammatical, as цвеће is collective and not in the genitive plural. (better: букет) |
| `embark_v` | започињати | ok | doubtful: Започињати is plain 'begin'; 'embark on a venture' is better упуштати се (у), and the hint is a noun phrase. (better: упуштати се) |
| `scramble_n` | успон | collision | doubtful: Успон is a plain ascent; a scramble is a hands-and-feet climb over rough rock, better rendered as пењање. (better: пењање) |
| `stimuli_n` | стимулуси | ok | doubtful: Стимулуси is a technical Latinism; надражаји or подстицаји are more natural, and the plural form is shaky. (better: надражаји) |
| `wooded_adj` | шумовит | ok | doubtful: Шумовит is rare and bookish; пошумљен is the normal adjective for a tree-covered hillside. (better: пошумљен) |
| `rarity_n` | реткост | ok | doubtful: Реткост is right and g: f is correct, but the hint стање бити ретким is ungrammatical. |
| `appliance_n` | апарат | ok | doubtful: Апарат alone is broader and more technical; уређај or кућни апарат names a household appliance more precisely. (better: уређај) |
| `hostess_n` | домаћица | ok | doubtful: Домаћица also means housewife; acceptable for a hostess welcoming guests, but the sense needs context beyond the hint. (better: домаћица) |
| `acidic_adj` | кисео | ok | doubtful: Кисео is fine, but the hint киселкастог means slightly sour and is a genitive form, misleading for strongly acidic. |
| `ludicrous_adj` | смешан | collision | doubtful: Смешан mostly means funny; for an absurd idea, смехотворан or апсурдан conveys ludicrous better. (better: смехотворан) |
| `negligent_adj` | немаран | ok | doubtful: Term is fine, but the hint 'безбрижан' means carefree, which is not the sense of negligent in one's duties. (better: немаран) |
| `explanatory_adj` | објашњавајући | ok | doubtful: Објашњавајући is a stiff calque with an active participle; Serbian more naturally says пратећи or појашњавајући for explanatory notes. (better: пратећи) |
| `reassure_v` | уверавати | collision | doubtful: Уверавати means to assure of a fact; calming fears is closer to умиривати (pf умирити). (better: умиривати) |
| `malfunction_v` | отказивати | collision | doubtful: Imperfective отказивати is unnatural for machine failure (usually perfective отказати); and отказати also means cancel or refuse. (better: не радити исправно) |
| `compel_v` | присиљавати | ok | doubtful: Присиљавати has a Croatian flavour; приморавати (pf. приморати) is the more usual choice in Serbia. (better: приморавати) |
| `constraint_n` | ограничење | collision | doubtful: Ограничење is right, but the hint недостатак слободе (lack of freedom) is inaccurate for budget constraints. |
| `unmanned_adj` | беспилотан | ok | wrong: Беспилотни is the definite form; the convention requires the indefinite masculine беспилотан, and it fits drones only. (better: беспилотан) |
| `tinker_v` | поправљати | collision | doubtful: Поправљати means to repair; tinkering with small adjustments is better rendered дотеривати or петљати, as the hint itself suggests. (better: дотеривати) |
| `reassurance_n` | уверавање | ok | doubtful: Уверавање means assuring or convincing; comforting someone who is anxious is better expressed by умирење or охрабрење. (better: умирење) |
| `dismal_adj` | мрачан | collision | doubtful: Мрачан means dark or grim; for dismal cold weather суморан is more natural, and the hint 'веома тужан' misses the gloomy sense. (better: суморан) |
| `timid_adj` | стидљив | collision | doubtful: Стидљив means bashful or shy; timid, fearful and lacking nerve, is closer to плашљив or бојажљив. (better: плашљив) |
| `nocturnal_adj` | ноћни | collision | doubtful: Ноћни simply means of the night; it works for owls but does not itself say active at night. (better: ноћни) |
| `itinerary_n` | план путовања | ok | doubtful: План путовања is an acceptable phrase, but the g: m note on a two-word phrase is odd; распоред путовања or итинерер suit better. (better: распоред путовања) |
| `mingle_v` | мешати се | collision | doubtful: Мешати се often means to meddle or interfere; дружити се fits socialising at a party, and the note lacks a pf partner. (better: дружити се) |
| `flask_n` | пљоска | ok | doubtful: Пљоска is a small flat flask for spirits; a water flask for hiking is more naturally чутура, and the hint is too vague. (better: чутура) |
| `agreeable_adj` | пријатан | collision | doubtful: Пријатан is fine, but hint 'угадан' is not a Serbian word; it should be 'угодан и спреман да угоди'. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: угодан и љубазан) |
| `prophetic_adj` | пророчански | ok | doubtful: Пророчански exists, but пророчки is the more common standard dictionary adjective. (better: пророчки) |
| `resonate_v` | резонирати | ok | doubtful: Резонирати is chiefly the physics term; the figurative 'resonate with readers' is better as одјекивати or наилазити на одјек. (better: одјекивати) |
| `allege_v` | тврдити | collision | doubtful: Тврдити is a plain 'claim' and lacks the sense of unproven allegation; наводити (pf навести) fits better and a pf note is missing. (better: наводити) |
| `concur_v` | слагати се | collision | doubtful: Слагати се is correct but plain and informal for a formal verb, and the note lacks perfective partner сложити се. (better: слагати се) |
| `rapport_n` | добар однос | ok | doubtful: Добар однос is generic and loses the harmonious understanding nuance of rapport; складан однос or разумевање is closer. (better: складан однос) |
| `rudimentary_adj` | основни | collision | doubtful: Основни means basic or main, losing the underdeveloped nuance; рудиментаран or елементаран fits rudimentary skills better. (better: рудиментаран) |
| `screenwriter_n` | сценариста | ok | doubtful: Term is right, but the hint 'писал' is not Serbian; it should be 'писао' (masculine past participle). (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: писац сценарија) |
| `closeness_n` | блискост | ok | doubtful: Term is right, but note 'g: f' is redundant because the -ост ending already shows feminine gender. (better: блискост) |
| `effortless_adj` | лак | collision | doubtful: Лак means easy or light and is too generic; it does not convey 'without visible effort'. (better: неусиљен) |
| `cynicism_n` | цинизам | ok | doubtful: Цинизам is correct, but the hint has wrong word order; it should read да су људи себични. |
| `innovate_v` | иновирати | ok | doubtful: Иновирати exists but is rare and bookish; the note gives no aspect partner, and the common phrase is уводити новине. (better: уводити новине) |
| `quirk_n` | особеност | ok | doubtful: Особеност is merely a characteristic; the odd, endearing quirk sense needs чудна особина or чудноватост. (better: чудна особина) |
| `upheaval_n` | превирање | collision | doubtful: Превирање means ferment or simmering unrest, not sudden change; потрес or преврат fits 'political upheaval' better, and hint says 'изненадна'. (better: потрес) |
| `sentient_adj` | свестан | collision | doubtful: Свестан means aware of something and needs a complement; it does not capture capacity to feel as in sentient beings. |
| `dispense_v` | издавати | ok | doubtful: Издавати means to issue or publish; for a machine dispensing samples, делити is the natural verb. (better: делити) |
| `conjure_v` | чарати | quarantined | doubtful: Чарати with pf очарати means to charm or captivate, not to conjure a rabbit; the sense and partner do not match. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; pf очарати → -) |
| `compulsion_n` | порив | collision | doubtful: Порив is a general impulse; for a compulsion to check the lock, компулзија or принуда is more precise. (better: компулзија) |
| `flicker_n` | треперење | ok | doubtful: Треперење is a continuous flickering; the example flicker of hope needs тračак or трептај, so the sense is partly mismatched. (better: трептај) |
| `oblige_v` | обавезивати | ok | doubtful: Обавезивати is acceptable for the law obliging, but note lacks the pf партнер обавезати and the hint is a fragment. |
| `revere_v` | поштовати | collision | doubtful: Поштовати is just respect and weaker than revere; the awe sense is lost, and the hint uses уважавати, which is also weaker. |
| `munch_v` | мљацкати | ok | doubtful: Мљацкати means to smack the lips while eating, a negative nuance; munching on an apple is better жвакати or грицкати. (better: жвакати) |
| `remedial_adj` | допунски | ok | doubtful: Поправни mainly means resit, as in поправни испит; for remedial classes допунски is the natural adjective. (better: допунски) |
| `elitist_n` | елитиста | ok | doubtful: Елитиста is valid, but it is masculine with an -а ending, so the note should give g: m and it is missing. |
| `poise_n` | присебност | ok | doubtful: Присебност means presence of mind; poise as calm composure is better сталоженост, which the hint already names. (better: сталоженост) |
| `magnetism_n` | магнетизам | ok | doubtful: Магнетизам mainly means the physical force; for personal charm привлачност or харизма is more natural, though figurative use exists. (better: привлачност) |
| `gusto_n` | ентузијазам | ok | doubtful: Ентузијазам covers only part of gusto; the zest and relish in eating is better rendered by елан or жар. (better: елан) |
| `lacklustre_adj` | блед | collision | doubtful: Блед means pale; a lacklustre performance is better rendered by млак or безвољан. (better: млак) |
| `riveting_adj` | занимљив | collision | doubtful: Занимљив only means interesting and loses the gripping force of riveting; узбудљив or фасцинантан fits better. (better: узбудљив) |
| `sprawl_n` | ширење | ok | doubtful: Ширење alone means any spreading; urban sprawl needs 'неконтролисано ширење града', so the bare term loses the sense. (better: неконтролисано ширење) |
| `sprawl_v` | излегати се | ok | doubtful: Излегати се means lounging lazily in the sun; sprawling across a sofa is closer to развалити се, and the hint is perfective. (better: развалити се) |
| `wayside_n` | крај пута | ok | doubtful: Крај пута is an adverbial phrase, not a noun, so the note g: m is misleading; ивица пута or банкина fits better. (better: ивица пута) |
| `fanciful_adj` | маштовит | ok | doubtful: Маштовит means imaginative in a positive way; fanciful implies unrealistic, which the hint нереалан contradicts. |
| `scathing_adj` | оштар | collision | doubtful: Оштар means sharp or harsh but is generic; for a scathing review жесток or немилосрдан conveys the severity better. (better: жесток) |
| `honk_v` | трубити | ok | doubtful: Трубити is the right imperfective verb for honking, but the hint wrongly mentions сирена, which is a siren. (better: трубити) |
| `docile_adj` | послушан | collision | doubtful: Послушан means obedient; a docile horse or person is better кротак or питом, and the hint is awkward. (better: питом) |
| `unoccupied_adj` | незаузет | ok | doubtful: Незаузет means not taken or busy; for an empty office празан or неупотребљаван is more natural. (better: празан) |
| `dissect_v` | сецирати | ok | doubtful: Сецирати is fine, but the hint 'препарирати' means stuffing or preparing specimens, not dissecting; hint should be расецати. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: резати на делове ради проучавања) |
| `telltale_adj` | речит | quarantined | doubtful: Речит does occur in речит доказ (telling evidence), but usually means eloquent; learners may find it opaque for telltale. (better: речит) |
| `connoisseur_n` | познавалац | ok | doubtful: Познавалац is stiff and rare; зналац is more natural, and the hint limited to art misses the wine example. (better: зналац) |
| `veneration_n` | поштовање | collision | doubtful: Поштовање is plain respect and loses the reverential sense of veneration; штовање or пијетет is closer. (better: штовање) |
| `wry_adj` | ироничан | ok | doubtful: Ироничан is only approximate; a wry smile is closer to кисео осмех or подругљив, so the nuance is partly lost. (better: кисео) |
| `heighten_v` | појачавати | ok | doubtful: Појачавати is a fine imperfective, but the note omits its perfective partner појачати. |
| `implicate_v` | уплитати | ok | doubtful: Уплитати means to weave in or meddle; implicating someone in a crime is better expressed by инкриминисати or умешати. (better: инкриминисати) |
| `justly_adv` | праведно | ok | doubtful: Праведно means righteously, a religious tone; for deservedly praised, с правом or заслужено is more natural. (better: с правом) |
| `overdraft_n` | минус | ok | doubtful: Минус is colloquial for an overdrawn account; прекорачење (по рачуну) is the proper banking term. (better: прекорачење) |
| `diverge_v` | разилазити се | ok | doubtful: Разилазити се is right and imperfective, but the note lacks the perfective partner разићи се. |
| `physicality_n` | физичност | ok | doubtful: Физичност is rare and sounds calqued; телесност is the more established word for bodily quality. (better: телесност) |
| `extravagance_n` | раскош | ok | doubtful: Раскош usually means splendour, with positive tone; the wasteful, unnecessary spending sense of extravagance is better suited by луксуз or расипништво. (better: луксуз) |
| `dramatist_n` | драматург | ok | doubtful: Драматург often means a theatre dramaturg; dramatist is more exactly драмски писац. (better: драмски писац) |
| `buffoon_n` | буфон | ok | doubtful: Буфон is a rare loanword; лакрдијаш is the established Serbian word for a clownish, ridiculous person. (better: лакрдијаш) |
| `rediscover_v` | поново откривати | ok | doubtful: Поново откривати is a two-word paraphrase; преоткривати (pf преоткрити) is a single established verb and the note lacks the perfective. (better: преоткривати) |
| `elude_v` | избегавати | collision | doubtful: Избегавати means avoid; elude as slipping away from pursuers is closer to измицати (pf измаћи). (better: измицати) |
| `oddity_n` | чудност | ok | doubtful: Чудност is rare and abstract; for a strange thing on display куриозитет or чудновата појава is more natural. (better: куриозитет) |
| `voracious_adj` | прождрљив | ok | doubtful: Прождрљив means gluttonous about food and sounds negative; for a voracious reader ненаситан or страствен fits better. (better: ненаситан) |
| `sedate_adj` | миран | collision | doubtful: Миран is just calm or peaceful; sedate implies composed, unhurried dignity, better conveyed by сталожен. (better: сталожен) |
| `credential_n` | легитимација | ok | doubtful: Легитимација is an ID card; credentials as qualifications are better rendered otherwise, and 'истоветности' is not natural (идентитета). (better: акредитација) |
| `glean_v` | пабирчити | ok | doubtful: Пабирчити is archaic or rural, originally gathering leftover grain; for gleaning information, прикупљати or сазнавати is more natural. (better: прикупљати) |
| `detritus_n` | отпад | collision | doubtful: Отпад means refuse in general; detritus is loose debris or remains, better остаци, so the match is loose. (better: остаци) |
| `envisage_v` | замишљати | collision | doubtful: Замишљати covers only imagining; the example means foresee, which предвиђати (pf: предвидети) renders better. (better: предвиђати) |
| `cleverness_n` | довитљивост | quarantined | wrong: Памет means wisdom or memory in general; cleverness as quick intelligence is довитљивост or бистрина. (better: довитљивост) |
| `hibernate_v` | зимовати | ok | doubtful: Зимовати means to spend the winter (also of people or birds), not specifically hibernate; note also lacks a perfective partner. (better: спавати зимски сан) |
| `personalise_v` | прилагођавати | collision | doubtful: Прилагођавати is generic 'adapt'; персонализовати is the precise term and the hint 'чинити личним' is awkward. (better: персонализовати) |
| `proliferate_v` | умножавати се | ok | doubtful: Умножавати се sounds like mathematical multiplication; множити се or ширити се is more natural for restaurants proliferating. (better: множити се) |
| `retrace_v` | враћати се | collision | doubtful: Враћати се alone loses the 'along the same route' meaning of retrace; the full phrase is needed, and no standard single verb exists. (better: враћати се истим путем) |
| `unmanageable_adj` | неуправљив | ok | doubtful: Неуправљив mainly means uncontrollable or unsteerable; for a situation out of hand, ван контроле is more natural. (better: неуправљив) |
| `quip_v` | шалити се | collision | doubtful: Шалити се means to joke around; quip is making one witty remark, so the fit is loose. |
| `unassisted_adj` | без помоћи | ok | doubtful: Без помоћи is a phrase, not an adjective; самосталан or самостално gives a single word for unassisted. (better: самосталан) |
| `beguiling_adj` | заводљив | ok | doubtful: Заводљив leans toward seductive and is rare; очаравајући captures charming and enchanting better for a beguiling smile. (better: очаравајући) |
| `plausibility_n` | уверљивост | ok | doubtful: Уверљивост is fine, but g: f is redundant since -ост shows gender, and the hint is ungrammatical Serbian. |
| `uninspiring_adj` | неинспиративан | ok | doubtful: Неинспиративан is a literal calque that sounds bookish; досадан or безвезан is more natural for a dull lecture. (better: досадан) |
| `untangle_v` | расплитати | ok | doubtful: Расплитати suits braids or plaits; for knots in headphones распетљавати (or размрсити) is more natural. (better: распетљавати) |
| `glum_adj` | тужан | collision | doubtful: Тужан is plain sad; glum implies a gloomy, sullen look, better суморан or натушен. (better: суморан) |
| `utensil_n` | прибор | ok | doubtful: Прибор alone is ambiguous (cutlery, equipment); kitchen utensils is кухињски прибор, and hint 'алат' is imprecise. (better: кухињски прибор) |
| `gushing_adj` | претерано одушевљен | ok | doubtful: A two-word phrase rather than one adjective; 'претерано' is explicit but clumsy for the effusive sense of gushing. (better: бурно одушевљен) |
| `clammy_adj` | влажан и хладан | ok | doubtful: Two-word phrase renders the meaning but is not a single adjective; лепљив or хладан и влажан as a phrase is more idiomatic for clammy hands. (better: лепљив) |
| `fiddly_adj` | мукотрпан | quarantined | wrong: Ситничав describes a petty, nitpicking person, not a delicate, awkward task; мукотрпан or замашан-ситан посао fits a fiddly repair. (better: мукотрпан) |
| `remorseless_adj` | немилосрдан | collision | doubtful: Немилосрдан means merciless, not lacking remorse; без кајања or бескрупулозан is closer to remorseless. (better: бескрупулозан) |
| `paywall_n` | платни зид | ok | doubtful: Платни зид is a calque; Serbian media also use пејвол, and the term is not firmly established. (better: платни зид) |
| `ventilate_v` | проветравати | ok | doubtful: Imperfective is right, but the note lacks the perfective partner проветрити that should be given. (better: проветравати) |
| `engrossing_adj` | заносан | ok | doubtful: Заносан means ravishing or enchanting, not absorbing; for a gripping novel a participial form like заокупљајући or узбудљив fits better. (better: заокупљајући) |
| `induct_v` | примати | collision | doubtful: Примати is too generic and the note lacks the pf partner (примити); formal induction is better rendered by уводити (у Кућу славних). (better: уводити) |
| `repellant_n` | репелент | ok | doubtful: Репелент is the correct Serbian word, but British spelling of the headword is repellent. (better: репелент) |
| `anticlimactic_adj` | разочаравајући | ok | doubtful: Разочаравајући means merely disappointing and loses the anticlimax idea of a letdown after buildup; антиклимактичан is more exact. (better: антиклимактичан) |
| `insubordinate_adj` | непослушан | ok | doubtful: Непослушан usually describes naughty children; for defying authority, as with a soldier, непокоран fits better. (better: непокоран) |
| `wizened_adj` | смежуран | ok | doubtful: Смежуран fits, but the hint has an agreement error (смањен и наборана); should be наборан. |
| `domesticate_v` | припитомљавати | ok | doubtful: Term припитомљавати with pf припитомити is right, but hint 'укрстити' means crossbreed, which is a wrong sense for domesticate. (reviewer said wrong, with no other term: kept; hint regenerated: припитомити животиње) |
| `immortalize_v` | овековечивати | ok | doubtful: Овековечивати is the right imperfective, but the note omits the perfective partner овековечити. |
| `blankness_n` | безизразност | ok | wrong: Празнина means a gap or void, not blank expression; the sense given calls for безизразност. (better: безизразност) |
| `adjoin_v` | прилегати | ok | doubtful: Прилегати means to fit snugly; for rooms next to each other, граничити се or налазити се уз is more apt. (better: граничити се) |
| `exhilarate_v` | бодрити | ok | doubtful: Бодрити means to cheer on or encourage, not thrill; одушевљавати or усхићивати matches the exhilarate sense better. (better: одушевљавати) |
